Under the Sunset

by landoscar

Summary

“Dude, Spiderman was opposite the building again! He’s been there so much recently, I’m starting to think he lives here or something.”

Oscar choked on a french fry, lightly patting his chest and grabbing his drink to wash it down. “Have you thought about the fact that he probably likes the area?”

“No! He goes there with a hoodie and a backpack, he’s probably on his way home or something! Could you imagine? Spiderman could actually live in the flats opposite us!” Lando exclaimed, turning his head to the window and he could slightly see the same building. The world now becoming suffocated by the increasing darkness, the sunset long gone.

Oscar pinched the bridge of his nose, “I think you’re reading into this way too much, mate.”

OR

Lando is obsessed with the friendly-neighbourhood Spiderman and Oscar is his good best friend that listens.

Chapter 1: Chapter 1

“Uh… is that Spiderman sat on the roof over there?” 

Lando’s eyes bulged as he scrambled up from his place on the floor, feet sliding as he hauled himself over to the window where Charles was peeking through the blinds. He managed to latch onto the window edge before he could crash into the wall, steadying himself before spying out the window alongside Charles. “Where ? I don’t see him!” He exclaimed, his eyes frantically scanning the surrounding buildings before landing on the red suited hero.

Spiderman was perched on the building directly opposite them, a plain black jumper thrown over his suit and a backpack next to him, kicking his feet like a child on a high chair. He seemed to be watching the ongoing traffic below, waiting for anything to happen. The sunset casted an orange glow over the sky and over Spiderman himself, his shadow looming on the cold concrete roof. It looked like an ethereal picture, Lando’s eyes beamed at the sight. No matter how much he sees it, he can never get tired of it.

Despite the hoodie and the backpack, the scene in front of him truly made Spiderman appear to be the great hero he is, casting all focus on the hero perched on the roof. 

It wasn’t the first time Lando had seen him sat on the roof as the sun would begin to dim. In fact, it seemed to be where he was most days at this time and Lando was always there to catch a glimpse. As the last two months passed and winter was drawing closer, Spiderman had always gone there just as the sun began to descend and the moon would rise. Lando was usually waiting by the window for his arrival, the time slipping his mind today as Charles was over and the sunsets were becoming earlier each day. 

Charles scrunched his nose in confusion, brows furrowed as he watched Lando’s near collision with the wall. “Why is Spider-Man here? Isn’t he supposed to be swinging around, fighting crime?” 

“Aren’t you supposed to be in your own flat instead of bothering me in my mine?” Lando retaliated, the Monegasque scoffing in return. It was no secret that Lando might be one of Spiderman’s biggest fans. He had been following him closely for the last two years when Spiderman made his first appearance as London’s finest (and only) superhero during his first year in the university of London. He was now in his third and final year of university, still as enchanted to see him as he was the first time when he was saved by the hero himself. 

Charles raised his eyebrows, “I do not want to hear you complaining when you invited me!” He had now turned to face Lando directly, but Lando was still too infatuated with the hero and his black hoodie. He felt an odd feeling in his stomach seeing him, he brushed it away. 

“If I hadn’t invited you, you would’ve turned up anyway,” he rolled his eyes, drawing the blinds back to get rid of the obstructed view to Spiderman. “You had a date with Max, of course you were going to show up. 

“You invited me to be nosey.” 

“It’s not being nosey if you were gonna tell me about it anyway.” 

“That is the last time I will come to you about anything, garçon stupide!” 

Lando fake a gasp of offence, still not breaking his eyes from the sunset glowing hero. “Don’t call me stupid in French, you bastard! I might be dumb but I can still understand you sometimes!” 

A smirk grew on the older boys face, “you were supposed to understand it” 

Lando scoffed, not bothering to respond. His elbows were now resting on the bay of the window, resting his head on his hand as he watched time pass. His hero is still there. “You still didn’t answer my question.” 

“What question?” Lando lulled.

“Why the fuck Spiderman is there!” 

The British boy shrugged his shoulders, “How should I know? He started doing this a few months ago. Just started showing up there at sunset, making himself comfortable for about half an hour and then he leaves.” 

“So you have been watching him?” 

“Course I have, how can I not? Tell me he is not the most interesting thing you’ve ever laid your eyes on!” 

Charles raised an eyebrow, “I was just on a date with Max, do you really want to answer that question?”

“Whatever, each to their own taste,” Lando grumbled. Max and Charles. What a pairing that no one could see coming (big on the sarcasm). The two had been dancing around each other since Lando met them, what started as karting rivals back in their youth ended up with them being absolutely smitten for each other and making everyone else around them begging for sweet release at their sickening flirting. Of course, Lando hadn’t been there for their first meeting (having met them both while they were third years and he was a first year) and thankfully didn’t have to deal with their rivalry the way that Alex, George and Pierre did. Charles and Max like to brag about how they were fated to meet again in London after meeting in their younger years. Lando can’t help but gag.

Suddenly, Spiderman got up from his spot and took his back bag, swinging it over one shoulder. He stood on the edge of the roof before allowing himself to free fall off the building then shooting a web to one of the nearby buildings and swinging away into the night. Lando could never be tired of watching him soar around the city, gliding through the air like an acrobat. “Huh, he left earlier today.” 

Charles shuffled so he was looking through the window again, just barely catching a glimpse of Spiderman leaving. “Perhaps he had a crime to stop? The life of a superhero never stops,” he suggested. He was probably right, it didn’t help lessen his disappointment however. Spiderman hadn’t been there for the last couple of days and Lando had been looking forward to seeing him. 

“Yeah I guess…” He sluggishly made his way back to the sofa where the paused F1 23 game was, long abandoned in favour of listening to Charles gossip about his date (and then getting sidetracked to talk about how his business was going, only to go back to talking about Max). 

His phone suddenly went off, he quickly grabbed it knowing only one person would text him around this time of the evening.

Oscar Piastri  

Coming back from my run now, you want anything from Maccas?

Lando rolled his eyes, how many times does he need to correct this guy?

Lando Norris

I’m not giving you my order until you either say McDonalds or Maccies you knob jockey

Oscar Piastri

You don't need to tell me your order, I already know it 

Knob jockey? What are you, 12?

Do you want any or not?

Course I want some what kinda question is this honestly

Anything else, your majesty?

Park was a bit crowded, been studying all day too, give myself a break

Promised to watch Game of Thrones with you tonight too, can’t let you down

You’ll end up crying again

I cried one time that you missed our movie night!! You can’t hold that against me forever 

Hurry up, I want my cheeseburger and McFlurry

Carry on with the attitude and you’ll be having a fillet o’fish

See you in 20

Please don’t

DO NOT GET ME A FILLET O’FISH

 

“What’re you smiling at?”

“AH!” Lando screeched, his phone flying before landing on the sofa next to him. He sighed with relief, he couldn’t have his old special gift breaking on him now. He whipped his head around to see Charles standing over him and looking over his shoulders clearly reading the conversation. The smug bastard had the nerve to stand there with a smirk on his face. “Have you ever heard of not snooping?” 

Charles scoffed before walking around the sofa to plop himself down next to Lando. “Is that not what you have always done with me? I have found you on my phone more times than I could count.” 

“I did that for your sake and you wouldn’t be where you are without me, so you should be thanking me!” 

“Can I not do any meddling for you myself?” Charles asked, wiggling his eyebrows. 

While it was no secret Lando was obsessed with Spiderman, it also wasn’t a secret that Lando may have a slight crush on his roommate. Slight may be an understatement. He quite liked Oscar. He liked Oscar’s dry humour, with his direct bluntness that goes hand and hand with his own humour. He liked how phlegmatic he was, remaining calm and level headed. He liked Oscar’s smile, when he would say something sweet to Lando. His laugh, when he tries to stay stoic when Lando says something stupid and he can’t help but laugh. His eyes, the way he looks at him and they way they sparkle, it should be illegal. His hands, the way they move rapid fire when he’s inspired to draw what he sees. His body, the way it feels under his own when they do their nightly routine of cuddling watching something. His Adam’s Apple bopping as he talks. His hair after a shower. His flushed face when Lando playfully flirts with him. His ass, when he walks away from Lando and he can’t help but eye him up and down. His thighs-

He’s getting carried away. 

Point is, Oscar to him was everything. His roommate, his friend, the person who stuck by him for the third year rolling. They got along like a house on fire. But he also made Lando feel like his pants were on fire. 

“You’re not meddling with me and Oscar.”

“In your dreams, Norris. One can only dream of me having the hots for them, wait your turn, there is a very long line.” 

“And yet someone bypassed that line very quickly.” 

Charles whacked him with a cushion quickly, leaving Lando defenseless. “Bastard! Don’t hit me!” 

“Anyway, how long do you plan to keep this going? Pretending that your feelings are not there? It will do you no good, mon ami. You are only hurting yourself. I believe you should do something soon, for your own sake.” Charles frowned, the conversation suddenly turning serious. 

“Keep what going?” Lando questioned. “I’m happy with what we have! It’s just a stupid crush, maybe a little bit of sexual tension on my side but I just need to get out some more! Find some hot girl or guy, get them in the room and get it out of my system.” He explained, waving his hands around. “I don’t need to do anything, he is my best friend-“ Charles feigned a false heartbroken expression “-and I’m happy I get to have him in my life at all. I’m keeping whatever I can of him.” 

Charles tilted his head, “Lando.”

“Charles.” 

“Just take care of yourself, oui?”

“Thanks for caring, dad.” Charles snorted. 

“You wish you had a dad as hot as me” 

Lando scrunched his nose in disgust, “you are filthy, and not the good kind!” 

They swiftly moved on from the discussion of Oscar, sensing that Lando was done talking about it any further. Time passes before they could hear the front door open and close, Lando darting his eyes to look at the person entering. 

He grinned as he saw Oscar, and the grin only grew more when he saw the McDonald’s bag in his hand, flying up from the sofa and running towards the food, “gimme!” 

“Hold yourself, mate. You can wait for me to take my shoes off, you scoundrel.” Oscar teased as he removed his trainers. He looked up to say something else to Lando before noticing Charles sat in the living room. “Charles! Shit, sorry mate I didn’t know you were here. Want me to whip you something up?” He glared at Lando, once again forgetting to let him know a guest was over.

“Non, I will leave you two to it. I must get going, I promised to give maman a call tonight before she heads to bed.” Charles hastily made his way from the sofa to the front door, pulling his coat on elegantly while slipping his shoes on, a red scarf wrapping around his neck. “You are coming to my Christmas party, right? Would not want you to miss this year’s one, you have to actually stay for one of them!” 

Oscar seemed a bit sheepish, a hand coming up to rub the back of his neck. “Wouldn’t miss it for the world, mate. Can’t say I’ll be too much fun to have around though.” 

“Shut up!” Lando lightly smacked his arm. “You’re coming, we can drink if you want and we’ll have fun, we’ll make up for you completely missing last year.” 

Oscar. He seemed to make many excuses for things. Over the years, he had missed out on many parties held by their friends, skipped most clubbing nights, he kept to himself but did his best to remain sociable with their friends (mostly thanks to Lando dragging him everywhere he went). He always made sure to have as much time as he could for Lando outside his schedule though, outside his busy life. Lando started blaming the fact Oscar was just a lot more introverted than others and he needed Lando to bring him out of his shell.

“He’ll be there, I’ll make sure of it.” 

“Good,” Charles smiled sweetly. “Right, I best go. Have a good night both. Lando, think about what I said!” He shouted on his way out of the flat, leaving them both standing in the doorway.

Oscar leaned out the door to watch him leave before closing the door. “What’s that about?” He raised an eyebrow at the Monegasque’s antics. “Actually, do I even wanna know?” 

“No, you really don’t.” He sighed, snatching the bag from Oscar’s hands and ignoring his exclaimed ‘hey!’. He jumped on the sofa, brought the coffee table closer and emptied the bag's contents. A minute later, Oscar sat down after removing his black hoodie. Lando eyed him up and down before, having to stop himself from liking his lips. Instead of focusing on his hair, his glistening skin and the pink flush on his face, he said:  “ew, you’re so sweaty.” 

Not what he was thinking, but it will do.

The Australian rolled his eyes, “I mean I did just get back from a run. What do you expect? I’ll be clean and pristine? Pass me my fries, dickhead.” 

Lando pushed Oscar’s items towards him, also grabbing the orange blanket and draping it over them both after noticing Oscar still had shorts on from the run. “I honestly expected a fillet o’fish after you stopped answering me.”

He had heard banging coming from Oscar’s room over the sound of his music and could see the door moving with it. Whether it was grabbing something Lando dropped, knocked into or even Lando himself, he was always there to catch it. He thought it was quite sweet, how observant Oscar was to notice and be quick enough to grab it.

Oscar choked on a french fry, lightly patting his chest and grabbing his drink to wash it down. “Have you thought about the fact that he probably likes the area?” 

“No! He goes there with a hoodie and a backpack, he’s probably on his way home or something! Could you imagine? Spiderman could actually live in the flats opposite us!” Lando exclaimed, turning his head to the window and he could slightly see the same building. The world now becoming suffocated by the increasing darkness, the sunset long gone.

Oscar pinched the bridge of his nose, “I think you’re reading into this way too much, mate.” 

“He’s there nearly every day, Oscar!” Lando pouts, his accent emphasising ‘Oscuh’, a barely there smile made its way to the Australian’s face. “There has got to be a reason he’s there everyday.” 

He shrugged. “Maybe he enjoys the view?” 

Lando sighed before going back to his food. There had to be a reason that Spiderman kept going there during the sunset. Why would he be there nearly every day right as sunset begins? Why would he be in their area at all? London was big, he certainly had a lot of area to cover, he could better preoccupy himself. Whatever the reason, Lando was determined to find out.

The Aussie finished his food and stuffed the rubbish into the carrier bag. “Just gonna pop to the shower, I’ll be back in 10.” 

This was their routine. They both attend their lectures, Lando takes some photos for his photography account and heads to the Daily Bugle to do some hours, submitting the photos he’d taken. Oscar will also head to his job at the library or to his internship at Webber Industries. Lando will go home, Oscar will go on a run. He’ll come home, they’ll order a takeaway or cook food together. They’ll eat, shower, watch a show and head to bed.

Max called it domestic. Lando agreed.

Oscar eventually returned, wearing a plain white T-shirt and some loose grey joggers. He climbed back under the blanket, reached for the television remote, changing the source of the television from the Playstation, beginning to flick through the channels absentmindedly. He stopped on the news channel briefly as he heard the mention of Doctor Octopus (the recent villain seen terrorising the city), the one Spiderman had been seen battling with in the streets as of recent. Unfortunately, everytime Lando caught wind of it and tried to show up to snag a photo- and maybe a glimpse of Spiderman, the battle had already moved or ended. 

Lando skipped past the channel before putting Game of Thrones back on and picking up where they left off. He had heard enough of the supervillain for the past few days, now it was time to focus on Oscar. Currently, they were on season 4, after Joffrey’s death and Tyrion is currently on trial for murder. Lando’s watched it all before, but he insisted that Oscar needed to watch and that Lando needed to be there to view his every reaction and answer any questions. 

The Australian turned to Lando, motioning him to move. He shifted closer until he was leaning against Oscar, his head resting on the younger one’s shoulder, his arm snaking around Oscar’s weight to pull him tight against him. Lando pursed his lips as a fuzzy feeling spread throughout his body, his grip tightening on the blanket. Stupid Charles and his stupid French language and his stupid accent-

“You okay, mate? Don’t think too hard, wouldn't want you to break.” 

Lando snapped back into focus, his head twisting up to meet Oscar’s face, concern evident in his eyes but hidden under a stoic expression. Ever the most expressional person, he nearly rolled his eyes, “yeah, I’m okay.” He said softly. They held their eye contact, sweet smiles exchanged as Oscar’s hand made its way to Lando’s neck, running a finger up and down the side of it in a soothing manner. 

“I did not kill Joffrey, but I wish that I had!”

“Oh my god no, rewind it, this is one of my favourite scenes and it needs our full attention!” 

As the evening drew to a close and they bid each other goodnight, Lando was nearly asleep before he heard rustling from outside his room. He waited a few minutes and it eventually stopped. With a sigh, he tossed his blanket to the side and climbed out of bed. Groggily, he crossed the room and threw open the door, knocking on Oscar’s door. 

Two times.

Silence. 

Two more times. 

No answer. 

He quietly opened the door, seeing the messy bed and clothes thrown on the floor, the curtains flying as the window was thrown open and a cold breeze chilled his bones. Lando’s teeth clattered and he dodged the mess on Oscar’s floor, closing the window. 

Just another one of Oscar’s midnight runs.

It had all started like this. 

Lando, a young boy from Bristol, looking for a fresh start had decided to go to the University of London to study Psychology. Which he would later learn would be the death of him. 

During that first year of university, where he shared a dorm room with 5 other people, he met Oscar Piastri. Oscar, an Australian exchange student studying Physics, who hadn’t a clue about anything relating to London and in a place he didn’t recognise, Lando practically adopted him on the spot. The first few months there, they had become near inseparable. Despite their rooms being right next door to each other, they could always be found together (mostly in Lando’s room for his PlayStation), often falling asleep practically on top of each other. They watched every show that interested them together, every movie, listened to album drops, planned concerts, everything they did together. Lando even learnt that even though he’s a Physics student, he was amazing at art, always sketching or painting his surroundings, sometimes even gifting Lando with a portrait of himself. He loved to watch Oscar, loved to just sit there with the sound of their shared playlist and watch him sketch away as he conjured up whatever was on his mind. 

They swiftly became best friends. 

They were inseparable- until they weren’t.

Instead of spending every waking moment together when they didn’t have any lectures, Oscar had taken up running a lot. In the morning? He’s off for a run. After a lecture? He’s gotta have a run. Just before bed after he nearly fell asleep on Lando? He absolutely must go for a run. Lando blamed it on the fact Oscar was now comfortable in the new city and no longer needed his emotional support friend. He’d always question Oscar’s weird schedule back then, why does he need to go on so many runs each day? Why does he sometimes need to go on more than three runs a day? It was always bizarre to him, Oscar said it’s just how Australians are. 

Lando believed him. Probably. But who was he to keep pestering him?

Oscar had also taken on a job at the library and an internship to help towards his work experience in physics, interning at Webber Industries. He had gone through a grueling interviewing process, he told Lando, eventually he had been chosen and since then they always gave him erratic hours to work. Oscar had said part of his contract meant they could call on him at any time to come and work a shift if they really wanted to, although he did have his own set shifts and they didn’t tend to ask him to come in too often. 

They were still close, still best friends. They both still texted each other at every opportunity and still hung out when they could. It’s why in the first few months of knowing each other, they decided they were going to share a flat during their second year. Maybe it was a quick decision to make, but Lando was absolutely sure that he didn’t want to share with anyone else but Oscar. The Australian was pretty laid back and kinda just went along with what Lando wanted, the British boy always did have a certain charm about him no one could quite resist.

Closing up to Christmas time during his first year of university, Lando and Oscar were ‘invited’ (read: heard about) to the party that George Russell was holding which was currently the talk of all the campus. Supposedly, he had thrown one the previous two years and this would be his last one since he was now in his third year. Who were they to ever say no to a party? Well, more likely Oscar doesn’t care but Lando drags him along anyway. There was no way in hell Lando was ever going to miss a party of that magnitude. 

Lando and Oscar had gone after catching wind of it and had gotten absolutely plastered to the point that they could barely walk (well, perhaps it was only Lando that was near black out drunk, he can barely remember, Oscar maintains the story that he was just a little bit tipsy). The night had been going well, he found himself taking control of the music, much to the bemusement of the friends he had made since being in university. He would soon lose Oscar in the crowd and of course, found himself getting into trouble without him. 

They had ended up playing truth or dare, with someone daring Lando to go to the roof of the flat block they were in and stand on the ledge for thirty seconds. In his head it was easy, no big deal, all he had to do was stand there for thirty seconds and be done with it. With a smirk and clumsily getting to his feet after using everyone around him to drag himself up from the floor, he made his way to the roof. Everyone was following, phones out ready to record whatever was about to happen. The friends he had made over the past month, Charles, Max, Alex and Pierre had hastily followed with Charles banging on about how it was not a good idea. 

It was freezing outside and he hadn’t been wearing anything but a T-shirt, goosebumps coating his tanned skin that was suffering in the cold winter days. He stumbled over towards the edge of the roof, only now seeing how high up they truly were. They were originally on the second floor of the 8 storey building. It was a very long way down, but there was no backing out now. 

He hoisted himself up on the brick wall outlining the edge of the roof, swaying as he did so. His balance wasn’t all that steady, but it was only 30 seconds. Lando turned his back to the city and faced everyone on the roof instead. As he turned to face them, he misplaced his foot, tripping over himself.

It was like a slow-motion scene from a movie, where a character is about to die and their whole life is suddenly flashing before their eyes. Where they start to replay the happy memories they have had throughout the series, never expecting them to be the one to perish at the end. His drink had long fallen out of his hand and went hurdling to the ground, his body soon following. Lando could hear the gasps, but it was too late. He felt himself free falling through the air and screwed his eyes shut. It was weird. People talked about a calamity feeling overcoming you when you were falling through the sky, but all Lando could feel was adrenaline and the overwhelming sense of doom. One moment he was falling, and the next he was against something hard.

His eyes were still screwed shut and the feeling of something hard against him made him scream out. It wasn’t until five seconds had passed that his screaming halted, realising there was no pain across his body. Is this what it was like to be dead? Did you not feel the life inside you leaving? Do you not feel the pain of what happened to you? He was suddenly very aware of his heartbeat pounding out of his chest, his breathing rapid and his body covered in warmth despite the winter night. He slowly opened his eyes, immediately met with a red mask, accompanied with big white eyes and black lines.

Spiderman. Lando had heard about him, he was all over the news at the moment. The masked vigilante showing up at every crime scene in London and sorting it before the police could even arrive. His first appearance was a month before the party, then he had sported a suit that looked a little like pajamas to him (but hey, he’s not gonna judge, he couldn’t get a super suit better than that) and now he was dressed with a much higher quality suit. He was everywhere, the news, radio, social media, you name it. Videos and photos of his sightings were everywhere. Lando hadn’t really had much interest in it, until that moment. 

“Uh… hey,” Spiderman began, holding a very distressed Lando Norris as it suddenly dawned on him what had just happened. “You gotta be careful on those roofs, you know. Very troublesome, not very safe.” Huh. He had a little bit of an accent, Lando couldn’t quite put his finger on it but it was obvious he wasn’t from around here. “You’re alright now, mate. Just stay away from the roof next time, yeah?”

Spiderman had lowered him down, allowing his feet to touch the ground again. As Lando touched the ground, his knees buckled, still feeling the effect of falling through the sky and nearly meeting a not so pleasant end. The masked hero still had an arm around him, pulling him before he could fall. “Woah there, easy now. Let’s sit down, okay?” He led him over to the bench outside the building. By now, everyone on the roof had run over to check if he had succumbed to the fall, instead noticing Spiderman was there. He could see a few of them running away, probably looking to come and meet the hero himself. 

“I-I…I don’t know what to say. Thank you, oh my god, if you hadn’t been there I would’ve just fucking died, flat like a fucking pancake on the pavement! Could you imagine my face becoming a pancake?” Lando burst out as soon as he sat down, his emotions suddenly catching up to him. He could imagine how he looked right now, probably a little dazed, hair sticking up in places it should not be, red cheeks and wild eyes. Maybe a tear here and there. Yeah, he was definitely crying a little bit.

Spiderman shrugged, “I mean, you wouldn’t really have a face if you had hit the floor.” 

“That doesn’t help how I feel!” 

“It should, because you’ve still got your face!” Lando gave him a pointed look. Spiderman stares back, and Lando isn’t sure how but he thinks he can see concern in the white eyes of the mask. “Seriously though, are you okay? How are you feeling? Do you need anything?”

“M’fine,” Lando replies as he rubs his face. “Sure, I just went flying through the sky but nothing I can’t deal with. Actually it seems pretty cool now that I've come out of it unscafed, at least I won’t ever need to do skydiving now!” He joked, wiping away the remaining tears that had fallen down his face.

The vigilante seemed to pause for a moment, thinking, seeming to be in thought about what he should say. Spider-Man finally snorted. “Wuss. Can’t believe you cried.” 

Lando snapped his head up to look at him. “Rude! You’re meant to be my saviour and here you are insulting me!” 

“I’m your saviour, yes, which means I can make fun of you, mate.” Spiderman chuckled, a playful tone etched into the words. “I mean, what kinda idiot stands on the edge of an eight storey building and doesn’t expect to fall?”

“An intoxicated idiot would.” 

“Yes, an intoxicated idiot would do that.” 

Silence overtakes them, both staring at each other before Lando bursts out laughing, throwing his head back as he takes in the situation. He never expected to come to a Christmas party, lose Oscar, fall off the roof, be saved by Spiderman and then be by a hero (which Lando is sure is a way to cheer him up… he hopes).

Wait.

He lost Oscar.

“Shit!” Lando shouted, patting around his pockets for his phone before realising it was gone. He looked around on the floor before seeing his phone stuck to the ground with webs. Pulling himself up from the bench, legs still shaky, he pulled the webs off his phone (a bit gross) only to see that the screen was smashed into smithereens. He turned to Spiderman, who was scratching his neck and refusing to meet his eyes. “So… my phone?” 

“Listen, you try to save a human and a phone at the same time!” 

“I’ve watched you on the news hold together a boat sawed in half, but saving my phone is the one thing you can’t do?”

“I mean I was a bit more focused on saving you, but yeah sure, next time I’ll make sure your phone lives instead.” 

Lando frowns at his phone before turning back to Spiderman. “I need to message my friend, Oscar.” He mumbled, a sort of endearing tone coating his friend's name. “God knows where he is, for all I know he was on the roof and saw that!” 

Spiderman seems to freeze for a second before quickly regaining his composure, Lando too absorbed in his broken phone to notice. “I mean, your mate probably would’ve come out by now if he did! I’m sure he’s fine, you can tell him all about it later.” His head whipped around to the door, Lando assumed it was the ‘spidey senses’. “You’ve got some friends coming now, maybe he’s with them?” 

“Lando!” Charles shouted out as he came running down the steps, throwing himself at the young Brit and gathering him in a hug. “You are so stupid! Why would you go to the roof? Mon dieu! You are very lucky you are not dead!” Alex came running forward too while Pierre and Max stayed back. George even seemed to be lingering in the doorway, keeping an eye on things.

“Dude, don’t ever do another dare again. You scared the shit out of us, I really thought we’d lost you for a second!” Alex exclaimed, a look of relief washing over his face once he saw Lando was alright.

“I may be safe.. but there was one casualty.” Lando carried out dramatically. “My phone.” 

Silence came over them before he could hear Max say, “you are such a dumbass, someone take him home.” 

“I’ll take him home, he’s clearly still wasted.” All eyes turned to the suited hero after everyone completely forgot he was there.

“Spiderman!” Charles screeched. “I am a very big fan of yours! Please, can you sign something for me?” He begged him. Max behind him seemed to groan and bury his head in his hands. Even Pierre grimaced at the reaction of Charles meeting the hero. Both parties were embarrassed to be watching him.

“Uhhh… maybe another time? I’m gonna make sure your friend gets home. Lando? Do you want me to take you home?” 

Lando weakly nodded, suddenly feeling exhaustion overcome him. It seemed his body was in between a state of fear and adrenaline, rolling between the two every minute or so. Yeah… he should really get home. Spiderman approached him, bending down to reach under his knees and pull them up, carrying him in his arms. Lando instinctively wrapped his hands around his neck, ignoring the way a blush creeped its way up his neck and his face. He quickly said bye to his friends (purposely ignoring their shit eating grins and their wolf whistles) before Spiderman shot a web from a gadget on his wrist and went swinging into the winter night. 

And when he was in Spiderman’s arms, swinging through the city with his eyes darting around the views (sometimes closing them before he could have motion sickness and throw up all over Spiderman, wouldn’t be the ideal situation), he felt himself growing more and more tired. The city buzzed around them, the Christmas lights flickering and the sound of carollers came through every so often until they reached Lando’s place. It was beautiful, to witness it the way the hero does, to soar through the city and experience everything all at once. He felt a thrill go through him. 

Spiderman dropped him outside his building, ready to rush off before Lando rushed forward and gave him a hug, not even thinking about it before his body told him to do it. “Thank you, truly. Fuck you for my phone but thank you for saving my life.” 

Spiderman chuckled, slowly returning the hug. It was sweet but short, enough to satisfy this new feeling of admiration he had. “It’s just part of my job description. Hopefully, I won’t have to see you again. Remember, stay off those roofs!” Lando let him go, not wanting to take up anymore of his time. He swung off into the night sky, leaving behind the young British boy. He looked at the street around him, people had been watching him interact with Spiderman. Lando looked back to where Spiderman had swung off to, and noticed something very, very big and potentially very juicy. Lando couldn’t help but stare and ogle.

Nice ass, Spidey. Lando grinned to himself. He may have found his new obsession.

He had been home for half an hour before Oscar came in through the door, seeing Lando wrapped up in a blanket. Lando had gushed to him for a solid hour about how amazing Spiderman was, how he fell from the roof and his life was saved, that he would’ve been like flat Stanley if it had been just a minute earlier. Oscar made him tea, gave him some snacks and made him shower, finally letting him go to bed once Lando reassured him that he was okay because Spiderman had been there to save him. Lando, so caught up in the thrill of being saved, forgot to even ask where Oscar had been at the party. 

It wasn’t until he was in bed, tucked away and reminiscing on his encounter with Spiderman that he realised he never told him where he actually lived. 

And when a brand-new phone had arrived to his flat with a ribbon on it and a note saying ‘I wasted my webs trying to save it and still failed, so here’s a new one to make up for it, merry Christmas’, it didn’t occur to him until years later that Spiderman also shouldn’t have known what his flat number was. 

Chapter 2: Chapter 2

Chapter Summary

Charles and Max pay a quick visit, while Lando feels a whole load of jealousy.

Chapter Notes

This one is a long one so i hope you enjoy! I also never realised how hard it was to write Spiderman as a character fml.

See the end of the chapter for more notes

It was finally the weekend, meaning no lectures or any work to do. Lando was someone that loved to laze around during the weekends, opting for a late morning lie in, fluffy pancakes for breakfast and watching trash television while he pestered Oscar to join in. Oscar’s idea of a perfect weekend was waking up at the ass crack of dawn (more like 9:00am) for his morning walk, return to the flat to cook Lando his fluffy pancakes, ignore Lando when watching trash television before he gives in and then he is off for his afternoon run or sometimes he has a shift at the library. Sometimes, their group would go out drinking and Oscar would occasionally join, although most of the time he ended up disappearing by the end of the night only to turn up at their shared flat hours later. Lando was used to it. If anyone asked, he would respond “it’s just Oscar”. 

Lando was very disappointed to find out that none of his friends were planning to head out drinking this week, instead spending their time with their partners and going on dates. He gagged at the thought of them all giving up clubbing for a date. Even Charles , of all people, claimed he was saving himself for the Christmas party (it was mid-November), Lando knew it was code for date night with Max without risking the relentless teasing from the younger boy. George was with Carmen. Alex was with Lily. Pierre was with Kika. Max and Charles were together. It was official, he lost all his friends to cuffing season. 

Except for Oscar. He may be stuck with Oscar. 

So that is where they find themselves now, Lando wrapped up in the orange blanket in their living room as he watches Extreme Couponing and Oscar is hovering like a dad who is pretending to not be interested in what you are watching.

Oscar had not long returned from his run and cooked their pancakes with a spacey look on his face. He had seemed a little out of it, Lando having caught him chewing his lip when the Aussie thought he was not looking. He could occasionally hear low mutters coming from Oscar, with plates banging and pots falling, now that was unlike him. Oscar brushed him off when asked about it and claimed he needed some caffeine in his body. The answer did nothing to soothe his concern. If he didn’t want to talk, Lando would not make him. 

It was not often that Oscar appeared like this. Out of the two, Oscar had always been the calmer and more collected one, the straightforward, emotionless one. While Lando wore his heart on his sleeve and expressed all emotions to the world, Oscar was better at hiding them or perhaps expressing them in a subtler way. Which is why it was worrying whenever Lando could see concerning emotions on Oscar, especially when he would sometimes come home and run straight to his room without a greeting to Lando. That was only on his really bad days, when Lando had to coax him out of the room with the promise of ordering pizza and letting him pick their next game or movie. 

But it was fine. It was Oscar, this was who he was. If he wanted to talk, Lando knew he would. 

Lando heard slight shuffling behind him, like the sound was being concealed as though not to be caught. He didn’t even need to look up to Oscar to know he was there. “Hey, Oscar~”. He twisted his head now to meet the younger boy’s face. “Finally caving in? I think you’ll enjoy this one more than Say Yes to the Dress.” He said with a smirk. 

Confirming his suspicion that the Australian was acting like a typical dad, Oscar moved so he stood directly in front of the sofa with his arms crossed over his chest, intently watching. His spacey look had left now, left with his pale glistening skin and a focused gaze. “So what? The shop owes them money now? That’s insane! How the hell does the shop owe them $0.10? Americans get away with so much, mate. I’m gonna have to ask Logan about this.”

Ah, Logan. 

Oscar and Logan met in their second year of university at a party that Lando once again dragged him to. They had clicked instantly, bonding over them both coming from different countries and being new to the London scene. ‘Barely new’, Lando had scoffed to himself, ‘you’ve both been here a year!’ Oscar had spent the rest of the party getting to know Logan before Oscar inevitably had to leave and eventually started hanging out with him. It had eaten into his time with Lando to begin with, sparking jealousy whenever the Australian brought him up. He had gotten pretty used to it by now, Still, he did not want to hear about Logan. 

Lando was never that fond of him, he just couldn’t get along with him. Charles blamed it on the fact that Lando was no longer the only person Oscar dedicated his time to, Lando blamed it on Logan being annoying. He voiced as much to Oscar, who always rolled his eyes, claiming he never gave the boy a proper chance. Lando worried that for their third year, Oscar was going to move in with Logan. Thank god for his good wits and irresistible personality, at least he beat the American in winning Oscar over for the year. 

Lando pouted and shook his head, anything to stop what could prompt Logan into coming over to their place. “Just watch it with me, you’ll get to see it for yourself!”

“Can’t yet, mate. I’m gonna head in the shower.” Oscar huffed and ran a hand through his hair, slightly tugging on it before dropping it dramatically to his side. Lando watched with his lips pursed before opening his mouth. Just as he was about to talk, Oscar spoke. “I know what you’re gonna say, I’m alright. Heads a little all over the place today, you know how I get.”

“Go clear your head, if you wanna talk about it we can mind.” Lando offers sincerely. He knows realistically that Oscar will almost always say no, the few times he had opened up over the years had mostly resulted in the Australian being a blubbering mess frantically apologising for his state. No matter how much Lando reassured him, he knew Oscar saw it as a last resort for his emotions. 

Oscar turned away from the screen to give him a soft smile. “I know, I appreciate it but it’s just my head going a little mad. I’ll be alright after a shower. Now, are we gonna continue Game of Thrones when I get out? We gotta finish it before Christmas.”

“We’ll never finish it if you’re taking this long to get in the shower.” Lando showed a toothy grin. Oscar looked at him with a smile and crinkled eyes, his stomach doing cartwheels at the sight. “Hurry up!” He launched a pillow at the other boy, Oscar catching it without missing a beat and throwing right back at him. 

“Okay, your highness! Geez!” Oscar ran off giggling as he made his way to the bathroom. He could hear the shower running and continued watching his show.

While Oscar was in the shower, Lando gave a glance over their flat. For two university students, they actually managed to find one that was quite decent, especially as it was in the heart of London’s centre. It had been the tenth flat they viewed before Oscar said he had ‘given up’ and the next one they viewed had to be the one. 

Fortunately for them, it was the one.

As you walked through the front door, the open panned kitchen was on your right and the living room was directly in front of you. To your left as you walk in was the hallway, leading to both of their bedrooms which were directly opposite each other and the bathroom right at the end of the corridor. 

They managed to scrape together what funds they had left from their student finances and working jobs to spend out on decorating the living room. The living room had a window on the wall facing the city (solely used for Lando’s spying), two beige sofas with a white rug in the middle of the room and a coffee table in between them; the television hanging on the wall. A smile crept onto Lando’s face as he recalled the memories of moving in, dropping the first television they bought for the wall. There was a bookcase behind the sofa, against the wall- not that either of them did much reading. Instead, they had filled it with souvenirs and photos of friends and family. 

It was small, sure, but it had been their home for the last two years and Lando was content. He was always content as long as Oscar was with him. 

A few minutes pass by and Lando can hear a crash from the hallway of their building. A frown made its way to his face as he moved to pause his show, curiously lifting his head as he heard the sound of shoes squeaking on the floor and the banging sounds getting closer and closer. Suddenly, the banging started on the door and he could hear a very annoying and familiar Monegasque accent through the door shouting. “Lando! Lando! Landoooo! Open the door!” He could no longer hear the shower running, abruptly turning off. 

Lando groaned, today was his day of rest! He moved himself from the sofa and stomped over to the door, throwing it open as he began to scold Charles. “You are a nightmare , Charles! I could hear you running all over the place- hey Max- you are going to upset our neighbours! Again! We already had a noise complaint.” He took a look at the almost-couple in front of him, a delighted grin and wild eyes on the Monegasque while Max stood there with his arms crossed over his chest with a bored look. “What do you possibly want so badly that you had to throw yourself into walls to get here?”

“Oh, nothing too important… I just thought you would like to hear about my encounter with your little obsession today.” Charles' grin started to look somewhat sinister, making Lando grimace before he realised what his friend had said. 

He gasped, eyes widening with excitement and his jaw dropping. “ Spiderman? You saw him?

Charles didn’t answer, instead he squeezed past Lando and made his way into the flat, throwing his coat onto the kitchen counter before standing proudly in the middle of the living room. Max begrudgingly followed him before addressing Lando. “I do not understand his excitement over this, he just nearly had an incident and instead of processing it, he ran straight here to tell you.”

Lando shut the door with a frown on his face, turning back towards his two friends with concern gleaning in his eyes. “Incident? What happened? Are you okay?”

“Is Oscar here?” Charles questioned, ignoring the younger’s questions. He wandered around their living room, heading to the bookcase and messing with the photos. 

“Uh… yeah I’m right here. What’s up?” Lando turned his attention to Oscar who had just walked out from the bathroom, his hair still damp from the shower, dressed in a pair of blue jeans and a forest green hoodie. How dare he stand there with water still dripping from him looking handcrafted by Aphrodite herself in a goddamn hoodie? If Lando had not been so concerned about Charles ‘incident’ he may have admired him for longer. 

Charles slowly turned towards the boy in question, a shit eating grin spreading across his face as he eyed the boy up and down. “I didn’t think you would be here so soon. You finished your run?”

“Yeah, I finished about half an hour ago. Why’s that?” Lando eyed his friends. Charles was acting… weird. He was always a little odd, but what was he doing? 

Max seemed to groan at the interaction, making a move for the sofa. “Out with it please, schat.” Lando also slowly made his way to the sofa, confusion growing every second the longer this went on. He situated himself between Max and the armrest. 

“I’m getting there, cheri! ” He shot back at the Dutchman, mockingly saying the endearing phrase. “Anyway~ I just wanted to make sure you were here to hear about how Spiderman saved me this morning! I know how much Lando loves him and by extension you must be here for it too!”

Oscar slowly nodded, there was a look in his eyes that Lando couldn’t quite work out. He eyed the boy carefully, he couldn’t read his body language but he could see the way his body had tensed. “Well firstly, I hope you’re alright, mate. What happened with- uh- Spiderman?”

Charles seemed to let out a light laugh before being his tangent. “Well, Max and I were heading out for breakfast to the local cafe. I was a little silly when trying to cross the road and didn’t see the car coming towards us! By the time I noticed, the car was so close that I froze in fear!” He was making wild hand gestures as he explained, pacing around their living room while Oscar stood silently still in the hallway. “I could do nothing, poor Max had to watch as a car nearly flattened me!” He cried out. 

Max shrugged lightly. “It’s true, I didn’t know what to do. He walked into the traffic without even looking.” 

“But then, our friendly neighbourhood Spiderman swooped in and swept me off my feet! He swung in and saved me just in time. I was so grateful, you know? So after we were across the street and I was safe on the ground, I went to thank him and…” Charles trailed off as he looked over at Oscar, there was a mischievous glint in his eyes as he continued on. “…and when he spoke, he just seemed so familiar. It was very comforting really, it just really felt like I knew him. He just gave off this calm eura that really pulls you in, a very direct approach but lovely. Maybe that is why the people like him so much! Maybe that is why you like him so much, Lando.” Lando was just becoming even more confused.

Lando was hanging off his every word, it was not everyday someone he knew had been saved by Spiderman. Unfortunately for him, he had not actually seen the vigilante in person since the day he fell off the roof and none of his friends had the pleasure of encountering him since that day either. He had longed to see him again, watching him from the window as he enjoyed the sunset. Jealousy coursed through his body as he listened intently, he just needed to see Spider-Man again. He felt like he had so many things to say and discuss with him (and maybe just sit and stare at him). 

He knew what Charles meant by the familiar feeling however, Spiderman just gave off this feeling like you had known him for years, very easy to speak to and there was something so calming about him (even if meeting him had shaken Lando up a little). He was convinced the boy had a secret super power where he could magically just calm everyone in the vicinity. 

Unbeknownst to Lando, Oscar had frozen and tensed at the sentence before gathering himself together. “Oh really?” His stoic voice said, a slight hitch to his speech, which could have been easily ignored, if you were not Lando. “You should be very careful, Charles. I’m glad Spiderman was there to save you, we wouldn’t want you flattened like a-“ he seemed to stop himself suddenly, his eyes widening slightly. What the fuck is going on? “- Anyway, the important thing is you’re alright, mate. Damn, you and Lando are so accident prone. We’re gonna have to start keeping you guys in those toddler leashes.” The Aussie joked, his hands bundled together inside his hoodie’s pocket. 

By now, Lando’s brain hurt enough to stop listening to the conversation, not understanding the hesitation and awkwardness from his roommate. He turned to Max, who seemed to have the same idea as him. “Lando, I don’t have a clue what is going on,”

He could hear Oscar and Charles bickering slightly in the background. He could bear the low whisper of  ‘how does he not know?’ before responding to Max. “Honestly? Fuck knows, did he hit his head?”

“It’s Charles, he was born this way.”

“Is he okay though? I know what it feels like to be… that close to something happening.” Lando, as much as he does like to joke about it, had felt on edge the whole week after he fell off the roof. The video of it had gone all around the campus, all over his social media and being constantly tagged in it by people he knew. To have to watch it happen again, knowing if it had just happened a minute earlier, it sickened him. It took a few months for the videos to die down and it took a while before Lando was comfortable going to such heights. It was fun to joke about now, but he understood it. 

Max eyed Charles, a soft expression crossed his face as he took him in. “He is running on adrenaline right now, I can tell. But don’t worry, I’ll be there when he drops.” 

“Seriously you two?” The booming sound of Charles' voice interrupted their conversation. “There is nothing you would like to say about what happened?”

Lando and Max stared at each other before turning back to Charles. “Uh- no? I hope you’re feeling okay though and I’m glad you weren’t hurt. Just goes to show how amazing Spiderman is. Isn’t he the best? I got to feel all of that chest of his when he brought me home before.” Lando gushed with a dreamy voice, shutting his eyes as he reminisced about the encounter. “I can’t believe you got to see him, I’ve been waiting two years to see him again!”

Charles stared at him, jaw dropped and baffled behind belief. “Mon dieu, comment je pourrais le rendre encore plus évident? ” He muttered to himself before rushing to the kitchen and throwing his coat back over his body. Max flew up from the sofa, following after him like a lost puppy. “You are an idiot.”

“Schat, I have no clue what is happening.”

“We’ll talk later, Maxie. You two-“ Charles eyed them as zipped up his coat. Lando now noticed Oscar backing in on himself, looking small in the hallway with his shoulders hunched and his complexion paler than usual. “-we are all still on to meet in the week, oui ?”

Oscar shook himself out of his shrunken position, regaining his composure as he addressed the Monegasque. “Uh, yeah I guess? Everyone else said yes to coming over so..” He brought his hand to the back of his neck, playing with the hair. Lando watched him closely, he only did that when he had what Lando would call ‘uncertain feelings’. 

“And you will be there too, Oscar? Will you be staying this time?” Charles seemed to be staring him down, putting the poor Australian on edge. Lando sent the older man a look as if to say ‘what the fuck are you doing? ’ But he seemed hellbent on staring down Oscar. 

“Y-yeah, I’ll be there.” 

“Perfect.” His voice seemed to purr. Charles checked his watch before he sighed.  “We still have breakfast to go to and Vettel’s Cafe will be stopping soon. We need to go before we’re late for the Padel match too! I would love to stay and chat more about it but we have a very busy schedule ahead of us. I’ll message you both later!”  And with that, Charles dragged a poorly confused Max with him out the door.

Lando and Oscar both watched after the door, an awkward silence filled the air. The British boy grimaced to himself, there was never an awkward tension in the room with Oscar, he didn’t like it one bit. He turned to the younger boy who seemed to be looking anywhere but at Lando. He didn’t understand what was wrong. Lando stared at Oscar, his posture had sunken again and it was only now that Lando noticed the purple bags under his eyes. 

“Oscar?”

Oscar looked down at the floor, his tongue rolling against his cheek and a sigh heaving in the thick tensioned air. “Hm?”

“I don’t know what that was about, Charles probably hit his huge head on Spiderman’s rock hard abs or something-“ Oscar seemed to let out a little chuckle, a slight curve of a smile evident as his face lifted to look at Lando. There it was, his Oscar. If he had to do one thing for the rest of eternity, he thinks he would choose being the Australian’s personal jester. He would never get tired of being the one to draw a laugh out of him. He would never get tired of listening to what played as music to his ears. To hear the sweet sound echoing in the room is all he could wish for. “-so just ignore him. But, you don’t look so good. You haven’t since you walked through the door.”

“I’m alright, mate. Just feel a little sick, a bit under the weather, I don't wanna worry you.” He mumbled, suddenly looking small in the hoodie. 

Lando frowned before making his way towards Oscar, stopping just before up and looking up at him, their eyes meeting as Lando examined his face. Up close, his eye bags looked worse. He had been working himself to the bone recently with his running, Lando heard him leave late at night and by the time he woke up Oscar was not home. He understood that is how Oscar works, but he does himself no favours. Yet, despite the exhaustion evident on his face, Lando couldn’t help but admire the pure beauty of Oscar. His hair was slowly drying without it being styled (and Lando had always loved it down despite the younger's protests), the moles littering his neck and part of his face, the little smile he reserved only for Lando, his brown espresso eyes that held a sparkle unlike any other. 

Lando’s hand reached up before he could even think about it, resting against the younger boy’s forehead. It felt a little warm, nothing too concerning though. Oscar’s body seemed to relax under his touch, still staring intently at Lando. “You don’t feel too bad. But you genuinely look exhausted, Oscar. I don’t know what’s going on because I’ve always told myself you’ll come to me if you need anything, but you need to sleep and take care of yourself. You’re gone in the middle night, you’re gone in the mornings, the afternoons and the evenings. You’re hectic and I don't understand why. Please, just give yourself a break for a bit, okay? I need you to take care of yourself before you drive me insane. You know what? Go to bed, fuck the show. You’re going to sleep now and you’re resting, got it?” Lando demanded. He was putting his foot down, Oscar needed this, he knew there was no way in hell that Oscar was going to help himself. 

The Australian had pink flush creeping onto his cheeks, it wasn’t often that Lando demanded something of him nor to see him so worried, it was usually the other way around. “I’ll be alright-“

“If you don’t go to bed, I’ll tell you who ends up on the throne.”

Oscar grabbed Lando’s wrist from his forehead lightning fast. “Oh buddy, you wouldn’t dare.”

“Did you just call me buddy?” Lando pulled a disgusted face.

“… I might’ve.”

“Okay, you definitely need to go to bed, c’mon.” 

Lando pulled his own wrist, making Oscar reluctantly follow after him as he headed into Oscar’s room. His room could sometimes look like a bomb went off in there. Clothes strewn across the floor, empty coffee cups stacked on the desk and remnants of his old sketches everywhere. On the walls were pictures of Oscar’s family back in Australia, he didn’t get to see them often living in London and it was hard to stay in touch with them with the time zones. It was hard for him at first, being such a family oriented person but he made do with the time he had to speak to them. 

All the clothes that usually littered the floor seemed to be picked up, probably shoved right in the wardrobe knowing Oscar. The Aussie let go when they were in his room and Lando closed the curtains, darkness descending upon the room. Knowing there was no way out of it, Oscar climbed into his bed and made himself comfortable. He must have been as tired as he looked to give in so easily. “Would you also like to be tucked in too, my lovely deity?” Lando teased as he strolled back over to the bed, sitting on the edge as he took in the sight. He almost sighed in delight at the sight, he looked so calm and peaceful already just being in the bed. In the corner of his eye, he could see the picture of them together at Winter Wonderland last year in a wooden photo frame, perched on his bedside table facing towards the bed. 

“I mean, I wouldn't object to it.” He mumbled as his eyes fluttered shut. 

Lando let out a loud laugh before smacking a hand over his mouth, Oscar opening one eye to glare at him. “Sorry, sorry! Ow! Don’t smack me, you hit hard! You’re a lunatic!”

A pillow had whacked him, making its back back under Oscar’s head once he was pleased. “Deal with it.”

“You’re so lucky I put up with you every day.” He retorts. Lando’s about to get up and leave him to rest, but Oscar’s hand had already sprung out  to grab him before he could even make a move. 

“Stay?”

Lando was sure he had hearts for eyes right now as he moved to climb into the bed. Oscar instinctively opened his arms, allowing Lando to crawl in underneath the blanket and curl up against him. He nuzzled his head against Oscar’s chest before resting completely. The blanket was brought up to cover them, keeping them safe from the cold air of the flat. Who needed the blanket anyway, when he was safe in Oscar’s arms?

“Always for you, Oscar.”

*

It was potentially a stupid idea. 

After days of deliberating on it, Lando finally decided he would venture across the road, to the building opposite his own and wait for Spiderman to show up at sunset. 

Spiderman may not even show up, he hadn’t been there for the last few days, but he was determined to find out more about him and why he always sat at that building. Ever since he had heard about Charles' encounter with him, he couldn't help but long to meet him again. He couldn’t stop thinking about their encounter two years ago and decided enough was enough, he needed to see the hero up close again. The plan was to head to the opposite building's roof and wait for him to turn up, to admire him up close and take some pictures for his photography account. Talking to him and approaching him was a whole other story, he could just admire him from afar.

Just a few minutes before the sun was supposed to set (yes, Lando was checking his weather app for the exact time), he had raided his wardrobe for a pair of joggers, a t-shirt, then headed into Oscar’s room before rummaging through his wardrobe too. They both had a bad habit for wearing each other’s clothes, Lando claiming that the Aussie’s hoodies were far comfier than his own and Oscar simply just took revenge by taking Lando’s favourite clothes. His eyes landed on a black hoodie, shoved to the bottom of the wardrobe, that’ll do. 

It must’ve been a new hoodie, he’s stolen enough of them over the years to notice. It was just plain black, a bit bland for Lando’s taste but it’s not his to worry about. Lando pulled the hoodie over his body and breathed in the scent of Oscar, his cologne and natural scent present on the fabric. He must’ve worn it quite recently. The fabric was soft against his skin and was nicely oversized. He lifted the collar up to his nose and mouth, his lips touching the fabric as he softly sighed in content. Catching himself in the act, he dropped the collar and pulled a face to himself. God, get a hold of yourself! 

He ran out of Oscar’s room before heading to the front door, he slipped on his fluffy crocs (a lovely present from Oscar, he wasn’t going to complain) and grabbed his camera bag, locking the door as he headed out. Lando sent a quick message to the Aussie, letting him know he’s gone out and begun his journey to the other building. 

Lando crossed the busy street carefully and looked both ways, thank you ! He may want to see Spiderman again, but he wasn’t about to do it with the Charles method. He made his way to the building which he had recently discovered was actually a hotel! This was their second year living in their building and he’s somehow only just discovered it. Oscar always said he wasn’t very observant for things such as that, Lando always argued with him on it.

As he entered the hotel, he took a glance around the reception area for any staff, it wasn’t too shabby of a hotel if he was honest. Lando wasn’t really sure why Spiderman would choose this building though, the exterior was quite bleak and the interior wasn’t up to par with others. Once he saw the coast was clear, he made a beeline for the first set of stairs he saw and ran up them before he could be caught. As he was halfway up the stairs, out of breath and hunching over with his hands on the knees, he briefly remembered spotting an elevator and cursed himself. 

Eventually (after many breaks), he made his way to the top of the building, seeing the emergency exit door to the roof. Lando pushed open the door praying it wouldn’t set off any alarms, relief rushing through him when the door opened with ease and he was met with the chilling air once again. He stepped out, cautiously moving across the roof. 

The sun had already begun to set and Spiderman was already there, sitting on the edge of the roof where he normally was, clad in a green hoodie as he overlooked the city. He probably had only just shown up, Lando knew how he worked when it came to the sunset. 

The orange hue of the sunset cascaded Spiderman as though he were the centre of the world, like the sky itself had chosen him as its subject. Lando found it quite funny. He had always prided himself on how he himself looked during the sunset, he was no stranger to knowing he was good looking. The red nature of the sky glistening off his tanned complexion, his blue eyes with hints of seafoam green acting as the ocean that belongs at a beautiful beach accompanied by the setting star. His dark hair with the fierce curls, his pearly white teeth and his sharp features. He knew that all these features pulled together when under the gaze of the sunset, he had enough golden hour pictures to prove it. 

But Spiderman? He belonged in the sunset. The red of his suit blending in with the fiery sky and the blue acting as the contrast. Much like how Lando’s eyes glimmer and represent the dewy sea, Spiderman’s blue conveys the deep ocean, all the mysterious that lies beneath it, never to be touched and unraveled; never to be discovered. It was one thing to watch him be basked in the light from his window, it was another to see it so up close in all its great glory.

Lando stayed back, now that he was there he wasn’t actually too sure what to do. He initially came to just observe him and take some photos, to try and figure him out a little bit, but now he was there and all he wanted to do was have the hero notice him. He wanted Spiderman to acknowledge him. Part of him wished he had found a crime and reported it on the Spiderman Crime Watch app now, although he realised at the time when the idea propped out that it probably wasn’t very ethical and Spiderman wouldn’t take too kindly to it.

It was fine; he’d take some pictures and be on his way.

He unzipped the bag and took out his camera. It wasn’t the best one around, not the highest quality but it was decent enough for the photos he wanted to take. It was a gift from Oscar during their second year of university, brought to him from the Aussie on a random Tuesday after the two had a lengthy conversation about Lando wanting to pick up some hobbies or explore some potential career paths. Photography had come up, his eye for scenic scenarios and capturing memories coming to life whenever he used his phone to capture the scenes. Lando had brushed the conversation off until Oscar approached him with a brand new camera, profusely apologising that it wasn’t the best on the market but that he wanted to help in anyway he could. Lando could’ve jumped his bones and kissed him right then and there. 

Thus came the birth of Lando’s photography account called Lando.jpg, where he captured photos of Oscar, sunsets, his friends, beautiful scenery, events near him and Oscar again. 

There were a lot of Oscar pictures on there.

He had quickly built up a portfolio of pictures he was proud of and submitted them to the Daily Bugle, a well known journalism company that had an extreme bias against Spiderman and his antics. Sure, it didn’t align with his own views but he needed the money. He had his interview and was hired the next day. That should’ve been a red flag from the beginning but again, Lando needed the money and it gave him something to do in the meantime. All he really did anyway was submit photos he took or completed the photo requests given to him, he thought of maybe starting his own blog to upload his photos to anyway. Probably wouldn’t make much money from it, but anything would be better than the Daily Bugle. 

Lando stalked a little closer to the vigilante, lifting his camera up to his eye as he moved. Unfortunately for him, he hadn’t noticed the orange, rusty pipe running across the roof and stumbled over it, thankfully managing to keep his body from tumbling to the floor (just about) and keeping the camera safe. The sound and harsh movement was enough to catch the attention of Spiderman. 

Spiderman shot up from his place perched on the roof and turned around to bear witness to Lando’s feeble- but successful- attempts to keep himself up. The hero stood on the edge of the roof with his hands on his hips and his head curiously tipped to the side. The white eyes of the mask moved, the white shrinking and the black lines widening in size as he looked at Lando in confusion. Last time Lando had seen the suit up close, it seemed like a cheaply made outfit pulled together by mere threads. However, the mask was obviously a lot more technical, the colours were vibrant as if they were pulsating through the suit and in general, the fabric looked expensive. He certainly knew how to go about upgrades.  

“Lando?”

The young Brit froze, embarrassment flooding through him as Spiderman eyed him curiously. All he meant to do was take a nice picture of the vigilante and yet he was caught red handed. “Uh…hi?” Lando winced, his shoulders hunched as he started to fold in on himself.

“And to-” Spiderman coughed deeply before continuing, his voice having gone down an octave. “-what do I owe the pleasure to for you gracing me with your presence?” Spiderman quipped with his accent that Lando still can’t quite place, he took a few tentative steps forward while Lando stood like a prey being eyed by the predator. “Come to share the sunset with me, mate? Gotta say, it’s quite a beautiful view from here.” 

“Is that why you come here every day?” Lando could have smacked himself in the face, he may as well have just said outright that he watches him nearly every day like a stalker!

Spiderman left out a light laugh. “You’d come here everyday too for some peace and quiet. You know, escape some of the loudness of the city. It’s a nice getaway, here I can forget I’m Spiderman and I can focus on something beautiful rather than the ugliness this city can have.”  

“That’s a little deep, mate. No need to get all philosophical on me.” Spiderman laughed at him. “And I mean, ask my friends and I’m sure they’d tell you I am the loudness of the city. 

“I honestly don’t doubt that- like, at all. Also, do you even know what philosophical means?”

Lando was slightly taken aback for a second before retorting, “You don’t even know me! In fact… How did you know my name? Wait, does that mean you remember me?” Lando was almost giddy at the feeling of Spiderman remembering his name. 

“Oh please!” Spiderman said. “As if I could forget the drunk idiot who fell off the roof and then blamed me for smashing his phone after I saved his life.” 

“You could’ve been a lot more gentle with it!” He protested. 

“Quit complaining, will you? I bought you a new one!”

Lando bit his lip, he had him there. “Which thank you for, by the way. I never got to properly thank you for it. It was a newer model than the one I had so it was worth breaking I suppose.” His laugh trailed off. He wasn’t too sure what to say now, Spiderman was just standing there watching his every move and Lando felt like a twirling piece of meat in a kebab shop display. As he felt an awkward tension start to rise in his body, he decided to make a beeline for it. “Anyway- sorry to disturb you, I’ll just-uh… be on my way!” 

He quickly turned around and went to make a break for the emergency exit back to the building, until he felt a weird substance grab at the left side of his body, twirling him around and bringing him closer to Spiderman, his body involuntarily moving at his will. They were only a few feet apart now, Lando started blushing like a child with pinched cheeks once he realised what happened. He let out a breath he didn’t realise he was holding, a mist leaving his mouth and entering the cold atmosphere.  “Did- did you just use your webs on me?” 

Spiderman shrugged, giving off the impression of oh-well and he yanked on the web a little bit, bringing him closer and closer until Lando stood before him. “I might’ve, couldn’t have my sunset watching buddy flank out on me now, could I?” 

A brief memory of Oscar calling him buddy flashed through his mind, he pushed it away before he could let himself think anymore about the Aussie, he’s sure his blush has already become a crimson red from both Spiderman and Oscar. “Thought you didn’t wanna waste anymore webs on me?” He cheekily asked, a grin growing as he tried to gain back some lost confidence. His nerves were steadily growing inside him, but Spiderman (like he always said) just had a charm about him that made talking to him so easy. 

“It isn’t wasting webs when it’s something I want. Besides, you obviously came here for a reason and I don’t think it was to enjoy the sunset.” Spiderman tutted like he had Lando all figured out. He knew he was easy to read, but was it that obvious?

“Well-” Lando began, swiftly removing the web that was on the jumper he was wearing. It was stuck to his hand, his face held a grimace as he violently shook his hand trying to remove it. It eventually came off and he continued, stopping himself from gagging at the odd feeling. “-I’m not sure how much you remember from our meeting, but I actually live in that building across there.” 

He leaned a little more towards Spiderman and pointed over his shoulder, the hero didn’t bother turning, crossing his arms over his chest. “Of course I remember, I couldn’t forget a single detail about it when you got a face like yours.” One of the big white eyes winked at him.

Oh my god Spiderman basically just said he’s attractive, how the fuck does Spiderman expect him to cope? Spiderman, hero of London, saviour of the people, defeater of Scorpion, Sandman, Vulture, Green Goblin and so many more just called him attractive. 

“Stop thinking so loud, I can see the gears turning in your head, mate.” 

The words pulled him out of his head and a sheepish grin grew on his face. “Right- yeah sorry. But yeah, I've seen you sit here a few times, you know when I pass by my window.” A little lie wouldn’t hurt, he doesn’t need to know the truth. Although he is worried that Spiderman has some special lie detection skills. If the rise of the black outline on his right eye is anything to go off, Spiderman probably knew he was lying. “I thought that it was a pretty nice shot, the sunset is beautiful and we got the city hero! I thought I could take some pictures? Maybe? Please?” Lando practically begged there, feeling close to no shame. 

“Is that it? You wanna take some pictures of me?” Spiderman asked exasperatedly. 

“I mean- I would’ve liked a chat with you too!” 

“… Is that not what we’re doing now?” 

“I-I mean, potentially?”

“Yes, Lando, we’ve having a chat.” Spiderman deadpanned. He put a hand to his face, slowly tapping his chin. “You work for the Daily Bugle, right?” 

Lando’s jaw dropped, he was stunned. In a great display of bravery, Lando pushed aside any nerves he had going into this and put on a smug smile. “How the fuck would you know that? Who’s the stalker now? I didn’t realise you liked me so much, Spidey.” 

“Funny, I never called you a stalker, you just implied that you watch me when I’m here.” 

“Right well, you’re always sitting outside my window! I can’t miss it!” Lando protested, he wasn’t about to lose to Spiderman.  “Now, how do you know I work for the Daily Bugle? Got a little saviour complex about me, have you? Keeping tabs?”

Spiderman started stuttering out words, caught off guard by Lando. He was proud of himself in some way, he’d managed to fluster the overly confident hero. Oh, he was really enjoying this. “Fuck off, your pictures are always in their papers or on their website, for gods sake.” He muttered the last part before turning to face the city on the roofs edge again. 

Lando followed him, stepping onto the edge as well. It felt similar to last time, except this time he wasn’t nearly black-out drunk and he had the amazing Spiderman by his side. As he admired the view, he couldn’t help but think he was missing the true beauty of London usually. Don’t get him wrong, London wasn’t the most appealing place to look at if you were looking at anything that isn’t its main attractions, but it had its moments in times like this where Lando gets to see it fade into night. Spiderman seemed to tense with Lando standing next to him, probably thinking back to the moment they met, similar to himself. 

“So, you like my pictures enough to always recognise them and to continue reading a paper that trash-talks you if Jameson has a say in it. If you like them so much, you could let me take some pictures then, hm?

“Then what’s in it for me?” Spiderman tore his gaze away from the city, staring at Lando. His nerves started creeping back quickly and he could feel his palms getting sweaty, the camera becoming slippery in his hands. His stomach felt queasy with the way Spiderman was looking at him with the large eyes. He could handle the talking back when the focus wasn’t entirely on him, but as soon as the hero even looked at him, Lando turned to putty. 

“What do you mean?” Lando gulped.

“I mean- I’m gonna have my pictures taken and posted in the Bugle where there’s bound to be some bad press about me. You’re also gonna have all the credit for taking the pictures even though I let you.” Spiderman drawled on, a playfully mocking tone to the words. “So what’s in it for me?” 

Lando paused. He hadn’t really thought this far ahead. To be fair, he didn’t exactly think he’d get caught tumbling over himself trying to take a picture. He tried racking his brain for anything, what the hell could someone like Lando offer someone like Spiderman? His mine went for a whirl before he managed to land on an idea. “Well, I’ve been told I’m a very helpful person.” 

“Oh really?”

Lando grinned to himself, although he knew Spiderman was still watching him and could see the bright smile. “Really! So why don’t I help you investigate Doctor Octopus? I can help track him down and find out where he is. Anything you need to know, I can help!” 

“Doctor Octopus ?” Spiderman exclaimed. “You aren’t going anywhere near him! What the fuck, Lando? He’s really dangerous, you can’t do that.” 

Lando was confused, why was he so protective of him? Spiderman wanted something in return and Lando offered him it! He was more than capable of researching a villain, thank you very much. He was a bit tech-savvy, he even had a whole list of items on what could be improved about the Spiderman Crime Watch app (he might just email that list over to Spiderman instead). Just because he was a little loud-mouthed and clumsy, didn’t mean the vigilante could render him off as useless. 

The British boy tried to turn to the side to properly face Spiderman, ready to berate him for doubting his ability. However, in typical Lando fashion, he ended up tripping over his own feet and sending himself leaning backwards right over the roof again. His camera slipped out of his hand and he momentarily sent a telepathic apology to Oscar for breaking the prized possession he had graciously given him. Damn, he seemed a bit ungrateful for breaking all these expensive items.
