“I think that’ll be great for you, genuinely. You’ll be doing something you’ll enjoy, plus you can do anything you like with your own business! We’ll all be here supporting you too.” George smiled at him while Alex nodded along with the statement. 

“Any clue what you’ll be calling it yet?” Alex asked as he reached for one of the half full glasses, joining George in sipping down their cocktails. 

“I was thinking of calling it Quadrant.” Lando smiled brightly, his tongue poking ever so slightly through the gap of his teeth. It hadn’t taken him long to come up with the name considering his lucky number is four, but he was still proud of it nonetheless. 

“You guys continue talking- I’ll go grab us drinks. Jagerbomb and a double vodka lemonade?” Oscar abruptly stood up from the table, Lando’s thigh where the hand had been resting feeling a wave of cold. 

Lando nodded quickly. “Make it two jagerbombs, I might need it.” He called out with a huff as Oscar walked away towards the bar and George pushed one of the fruity cocktails his way.

The three continued talking while Lando nursed himself on the fruity cocktail, humming with delight at the sweetness masking the taste of alcohol. Eventually, he found himself walking towards the dance floor with the two boys, joining the rest of their friends. They had formed a circle in the middle of the space, looking like they were the main event. The strobe lights filtered around them and the speakers bounced as the bass boomed through the system. The DJ messed with some dials and Lando knew he’d eventually find himself up there, he was just biding his time. 

Lando looked around the circle as they slotted themselves in, Alex and George joining their girlfriends while Lando pushed through Daniel and Yuki. He noticed immediately that Max and Charles were missing. 

“Don’t even ask, mate.” Daniel interrupted his thoughts and threw his arm around Lando’s shoulders, pulling him into his side. The music was loud, causing him to shout loudly in his ear. “They’ve definitely gone to fuck somewhere.” 

Pierre turned from his girlfriend Kika and whacked Daniel on the arm, the Australian recoiled with an exaggerated noise of pain and let go of Lando. “They are not fucking! For once they’re actually being sickeningly sweet.” Pierre rolled his eyes. 

“Can we not talk about my brother fucking Max, please?” A voice broke through the loud music, coated with an even thicker accent than his brother. Arthur and Logan approached the group, a sway in the Monegasque’s step as the sea of people seemed to part around him. Logan looked to be following him like a lost puppy. “If you must know- they’re out on the balcony.” He pointed towards the back of the room, where the two mentioned could be seen through the glass balcony doors.

“Any idea what they’re doing?” Alex asked. 

“Charles is asking Max to be his boyfriend.” Arthur shrugged as though the answer were obvious. 

Silence filled the air for a few beats as they all stood there awkwardly, the sound of Pitbull in the background circling around them. Daniel spoke up, a puzzled expression on his face. “…I thought they were already together?” 

“I can’t deny it and let him look like a fool, I thought they were too.” George confessed and put his hands up in the air in defense. 

Suddenly, they all burst out in various conversations about whether or not they were actually together not. They were all trying to shout at each other over the music and Lando rubbed his forehead with the palm of his hand.

God, he needed another drink.

Just as he thought to himself about how much alcohol would help him right now, Lando felt a foot nudge his calf. Swiftly turning to look at the culprit, he laughed loudly when he saw Oscar trying to hold four drinks in his hands. 

“Will you drink your jagerbombs already? My hands are killing.” Oscar whined, his fingers loosening on the drinks the longer he held them. 

Lando decided to put him out of his misery, taking the two shots and throwing them back in quick succession. He hid his grimace well as the sharp burn of the drink lingered at the back of his throat, he licked his lips to clear it of the sticky substance before running to a table to put the empty shot glasses down. Once he came back, Oscar was quick to hand him his other drink. 

“What’s going on?” Oscar asked curiously as he watched the unfolding scene of everyone shouting to each other over the booming music. They were nearly closed out of the circle now, all too busy arguing to notice Oscar’s arrival.

Lando took a big swig of his drink, his body shuddering at the taste of strong vodka. “Arthur just dropped the bombshell that Charles is asking Max out tonight and apparently everyone thought they were already together.” 

“I mean… they have already been acting like they are.” Oscar shrugged his shoulders, his lips touching the glass tentatively before taking a small sip. 

“Come on, you got some catching up to do!” 

“Absolutely not, one of us has to actually make sure we both get home tonight.” 

“You have to actually stay here for that to happen, mate.” Lando raised his eyebrows in a challenge, waiting for the inevitable response of him having to leave. 

Oscar sighed and pinched the bridge of his nose before addressing the Brit. “I’m staying, don’t worry. You wanted me all to yourself tonight? You’ve got me.” He said with a slight smirk pulling at the corner of his mouth. Oscar’s arm wrapped around his torso, his fingers gently pushing against him, pulling them slowly together. 

Before Lando could allow himself to become flustered and embrace the touch, he heard the gasps of their friends next to them. He turned towards where they were all looking and laughed when he saw Max entering the building with a small fond smile, carrying Charles' bridal style through the doors. 

“We’re getting hitched!” Charles exclaimed across the room to their friends, their friends all looking over in shock. Arthur’s drink had slid out of his hand and fallen to the ground. 

Max looked at Charles with an exasperated expression before dropping the Monégasque out of his grip. The younger of the two yelped and quickly wrapped his arms around Max’s neck, yanking them both towards the ground and sending them tumbling. Once they landed in a rather awkward position, Max shouted over the speakers. “We are not getting hitched, he’s just my boyfriend.” He rolled his eyes and looked at Charles, unable to hide his loving look as he pulled them both up from the floor. 

Charles was a giggling mess, clearly already having a few too many most likely before the party had even started. He let himself be manhandled as Max dragged him towards the centre of the dance floor, grinding against each other shamelessly. The rest of their friends looked away in disgust, although it did cause them to loosen up a little and start dancing to the beat of the music, a few of them making their way over to the shameless couple. 

Lando felt immense pride blooming in his chest for his two friends managing to finally get their shit together. He made a mental note to himself to congratulate them later, after they’d had their fun of course. 

Arthur groaned and threw his head back, picking up the plastic cup that was now empty, the liquid pooling around their feet. “Great- Charles' inability to have any self control wasted yet another drink of mine.” 

“Come on, I’ll grab you another drink.” Logan offered, his hand reaching out towards Arthur. Lando whipped his head around to Oscar in shock, who looked equally as surprised as him. Arthur tentatively took Logan’s hand, the two walking away towards the bar. 

“What the fuck was that?” Lando squeaked.

“I think Logan is finally acting on his crush.” Oscar said, his eyes still trailing after the two. “I honestly didn’t know he even had it in him.” 

“What the fuck do the Leclerc boys have that I don’t?” Lando whined to himself. “How are they both getting lucky tonight?” 

Oscar raised his eyebrow with an amused glint in his eyes. “Do you really want Max or Logan?” 

“… No.” 

“Exactly. Now, let’s go dance before we gotta watch everyone suck each other’s faces tonight.” Oscar grimaced.

The two made their way further into the dance floor, being carefully led by Oscar’s guiding hands on his back, already feeling the slight effect of the burning alcohol as it travelled through him. Lando winced to himself, he did have a tendency to overdo it too quickly. 

As they rejoined the group in the middle of the dance floor, Oscar felt a wave of self-consciousness lay over him like a weighted blanket, his limbs moving awkwardly as he shuffled his feet a little. Meanwhile, Lando was swaying seamlessly to the rhythm of the music, bouncing on the balls of his feet slightly and his head lolling to the side. The younger watched Lando in awe, a deep fascination evident in his eyes as Lando rolled his hips subtly, throwing a playful wink to Oscar. His cheeks drew red as a blush as warm as a wildfire instantly grew.

Lando was always the one to watch at the party, the main entertainment and the enticing drunk figure, drawing everyone’s attention. Every move he made was mesmerising and Oscar never wanted to look away. 

“Oscar!” Lando giggled over the thumping sound of the bass. The Brit’s hands were still exploring his body, moving up and down himself sensually as he let the music control his movements. “You need to loosen up! Wiggle those hips, baby!”

“There will be no wiggling of hips from me tonight.” Oscar snorted, but allowed himself to move a little more fluidly. “I’ll leave all of that to you.” He gave a curt nod to the pouting boy opposite him. 

Lando threw his head back before moving his hands away from himself, landing on the hips of Oscar. The younger boy froze mid-movement while Lando smirked in delight. He knew exactly what he was doing, pulling every trick in the book out to fluster Oscar. Feeling himself, rolling his hips, winking and touching him, Lando was well aware of the power he held over Oscar when he was drinking, his confidence only growing the more he drank. And this- this was only tipsy Lando. 

God, drunk Lando was a menace compared to what he was doing now. 

“Now, Oscar, when I push on your hip, you move it, got it?”

“Lando-”

“Oscar~” He sang over the music, grinning in delight as he pulled himself closer to the Aussie. Sober Lando’s brain would’ve short-circuited the moment their chests were nearly touching, tipsy Lando couldn’t get enough of it. “Just move with me, let me be your master.”

“Master?” Oscar shrieked, his eyes lifting from where the older boy’s hands rested on his hips to Lando’s eyes. The Brit was cackling to himself, his grip tightening on Oscar’s hips as his eyes crinkled.  

“Okay- not the word I was looking for. Uh- I’ll be your mentor. Teacher? You know what? Just follow my lead before I say something stupid.” Lando managed to draw out in between his laughter, Oscar letting out a light chuckle. 

“Every word out of your mouth is stupid.” Oscar retorted. 

“And you like every stupid word that falls out of my mouth.” Lando fired back quickly, his confidence blooming further.  

“I do.” Oscar slowly nodded, as if he couldn’t believe he was admitting it himself. This- it felt like a constant game with each other, over who could affect the other the most with their actions. Who could be the one to pull out of it? Their constant back and forth, both toeing the line, the barrier between them. Unlucky for Oscar, Lando was usually the winner.

Although, the Australian boy did put up a good fight when he wanted to. Which is exactly what he was doing now.

Just as Lando teetered on the edge of pressing for more, teasing him any further, Oscar surprised him by detaching Lando’s tight grip on the younger’s waist. For a fleeting moment, Lando frowned and was ready to take the rejection with stride, he knew when to take no for answer from Oscar. However, Oscar moved their hands together until they reached the younger’s neck, wrapping them around them. 

Lando adjusted his fingers on instinct, intertwining them around the taller boy’s neck. His head rose, meeting the soft gaze of Oscar, a small smile playing on the Aussie’s lips. As if guided by a force of nature, Oscar’s hands filtered down to Lando’s own waist, resting there as though they belonged there. Even with the tipsy confidence, Lando’s brain still turned to mush at the soft touch from Oscar. Sure- Oscar was bigger on physical touch when they were alone, but that was behind closed doors, away from the watchful eyes 

But this? This was new. 

He wasn’t about to start complaining when Oscar was looking at him like he hung the stars in the sky. 

“If we’re doing this, we’re doing it my way.” Oscar said with a stern voice, making Lando feel weak in the knees. He cleared his throat before raising his head properly, his confidence had wavered but it was making a steady comeback now. 

“Are you ready to follow my lead, Piastri?” Lando asked, his body starting to move with the beat of the music again. 

“When will I ever not follow your lead, Norris?”

The two moved with each other as the party roared around them, Oscar’s movements were no longer so stiff and felt the magnetic pull of Lando’s energy all around him. Between the unspoken feelings Lando harboured and the loud bass of the music, Lando found himself immersed in the body of Oscar. Lando bit his lip as he watched Oscar move, looking down between them to see the decreasing gap between them coming to a close until they were flush against each other. Their dance was hidden between everyone bouncing around them and the neon lights illuminating the place, transfixed only with each other. 

The party’s vibrant energy surged on, their friends had soon enough interrupted their little one on one dancing as they formed the circle around them, turning what once felt like an intimate moment into a communal spectacle. As the night unfolded, the room started to sway with the music and a dizzy haze took over his perception. The cycle of emptying one drink only to find another one shoved into his hand quickly became a blur, despite Oscar being there to tell him to pace himself. Each time he took a long swig however, he would spill it down himself and break down the stern look Oscar harboured. 

Lando may have purposely spilled it a few times just to hear Oscar laugh. His laugh was like a sweet melody to his ears, could you blame him?

He left the dance floor some time ago to grab himself another drink, feeling the dire need to drown out any of the past emotions and memories he was feeling before the party. Lando stumbled his way there, doing a few shots with people he’d never met and grabbing himself his drink before making his way back to the dance floor. 

The party truly came alive as the time went on, the air was alive with the scent of alcohol, the rhythmic beats of the music and people poorly singly along to whatever song was playing. The dance floor was packed with most people from the party now, all congregating there after an apparent British classic was played. 

Lando would’ve joined in with the dancing too, if he could just find Oscar. 

Eventually, he navigated through the thick crowd of sweaty bodies and hot air before finally reaching his friends. He scanned the circle for Oscar, disappointment taking over him when he couldn’t spot the brown haired Aussie. He leaned into the side of someone, his vision too blurry to see who was around him. 

“Do you mind?” A voice shouted just clear enough to be heard in his ear. Lando titled his head to look up and saw Max staring back at him with his brows furrowed. 

“Oscarrrrr?” Lando slurred while wobbling around, nearly falling over his own feet.

“Do I fucking look like Oscar?” Max exclaimed, but his arm reached up and around Lando’s shoulders, keeping him upright as everyone began to go wild over the next song playing. They were pushed from behind a little by someone jumping up and down, Max quickly shot them a cold-blooded glare before they could do it again. “Where have you been?”

“Just- uhhhh, getting a drink.” Lando responded slowly, his head turning to look back at the crowd as he frantically searched for Oscar. “Where’s Osc?”

Charles- who was on the other side of Max-  suddenly perked up at the mention of the Australian, interrupting their conversation. “Is Oscar gone?” He asked in disbelief, a look of frustration flashing over his face as he addressed the two boys. 

Lando pouted, his bottom lip wobbling as felt his eyes getting watery. Not only could he be an over confident drunk, he was also quite the emotional one. “He’s gone? But- he promised me! Oscar promised he’d stay for me.” He whined loudly, his knees buckling under the weight of his flailing body. 

Max cursed loudly in Dutch as his body was nearly dragged to the floor, however he managed to keep them both upright. “I’m sure he’s still here, Lando. If he promised to stay, he’ll be here.” Max shot a glare at Charles over his shoulder as he adjusted Lando to stand up straight. 

The Monegasque pursed his lips and gave Max a silent look. “As much as we want him here, if he did leave then I’m sure he’d have a good reason why, especially for breaking a promise to you.” Charles reassured him, coming around from Max’s otherside to join Lando. 

“What’s up, dickheads?” Daniel came pouncing over to them, two big beer bottles in both of his hands and threw one of his arms around Charles. “This is a party, why do you all look miserable?” He asked, his accent thicker than usual as the words slightly slurred out of his mouth. 

“Oscar.” Lando stated, crossing his arms over his chest. 

“Have you seen Oscar?” Max looked at Daniel with a hopeful look. 

“Yeah. I just saw him over there.” Lando gasped loudly and his head whipped up to look where Daniel was pointing with his free hand. A wave of dizzy sickness washed over him from the sudden movement, but he was still excited to see Oscar. 

His eyes landed on the Australian across the dance floor, he was dancing and laughing, having fun. Neon lights flew over his face and illuminated his glistening eyes and the moles littered across his face and neck. Lando hummed with satisfaction at knowing Oscar was still at the party. Lando watched him intently, like his gaze would burn a hole through him any moment, his mind screaming Oscar Oscar Oscar Oscar- 

Wait. Who was he with?

“Who the fuck is that?” Lando shouted angrily and suddenly his body was moving with no problem, like all the alcohol inside him evaporated and all that was left was a seething anger for the two people in front of him. He felt his face turning red as the blood boiled beneath his skin. There Oscar was, dancing with a brunette girl dressed in a short white dress. 

“No clue, mate. She just came up to Oscar a few minutes ago and asked him for a dance.” Daniel shrugged his shoulders and took a big swig of his beer. Max and Charles gave each other a quick worried glance as Lando glared at Oscar and the girl. 

“…And he said y-yes?” Lando asked through gritted teeth, a turmoil of emotions rising within him. 

“Uh-” Daniel looked at Charles for support, who in return gave him a jab in the ribs with his finger. Daniel yelped and rubbed the sore spot before sighing. “He said yes but honestly mate, I wouldn’t look into it too deeply! He was probably just passing time while he waited for you.”

Lando in his drunken state couldn’t accept it for an answer. He watched with hazy eyes as Oscar shared laughs with the girl, both of them twirling around each other in the sea of people. The knot of jealousy roused within him, the feeling unsettling him and feeling discomfort grow. 

Jealousy wasn’t a feeling he often felt anymore, not since he stopped worrying about Logan being in the picture. He never usually had to worry much about Oscar in these settings, he either followed Lando around or he wasn’t there for these things to happen. It was an unexpected jolt to the system especially with the alcohol currently clouding his judgement. With the moments they had shared up until then, with the warmth and love that overwhelmed him at the time, it all began to feel like nothing as he watched Oscar share that same moment with a random girl. 

The jealousy loomed over him like an unwelcome guest, and jealousy makes people do some… odd things. 

In the height of his intense emotions, Lando turned to Daniel with a determined look. He removed Max’s arm from his shoulder and grabbed the older Australian by the front of his shirt. “Hey Danny-” Lando hiccuped. “How would youuuu feel about helping me out h-here?” Lando smirked with the anger still evident in his eyes, occasionally glancing over to see Oscar still dancing with the girl. His speech was still slurred despite feeling like he’d sobered.  

“I’m not getting involved in whatever weird foreplay you two have going on.” Daniel protested and grabbed Lando’s hand, loosening the grip on his shirt. “Why don’t you two just- I don’t know, talk to each other?”

“Fuck Oscar, fuck you-” Hiccup. “-And fuck this party!” Lando shouted and turned on his heel, about to take a step forward to leave them. 

Daniel rolled his eyes and groaned, tightening his grip on Lando’s hand to keep him in place. “If I do this for you, you fucking owe me. There's a girl here I was looking to impress tonight, nothing too extreme please.”

Lando beamed with a mischievous look plastered on his face, feeling evil as he dragged Daniel away from Max and Charles, looking to put on a proper show. Daniel looked over his shoulder with a look of panic.

“This is a very bad idea, Lando!” Max shouted, cupping his hand by his mouth as he watched Daniel be dragged away with a face saying ‘ god help me’. 

“They are so fucking stupid.” Charles muttered next to him. “Seriously, I want to bash their heads together and lock them in a room until they just talk.”

“They’re not any worse than we were, schatje . Besides, it’s quite entertaining watching them.”

Charles looked over at Max with a shocked expression. “No- no, I think they’re much worse, at least we did something about it.” Charles said and swirled his drink around, the little remnants of it swishing in the glass. The sound of the DJ speaking interrupted his thoughts as he announced that they were now taking karaoke requests. A smile grew widely on his face as a wicked thought overtook him. “We can’t lock them in a room, but I know something else that they can do. I have the perfect plan.”

Max huffed and wrapped an arm around Charles’ torso, nuzzling his face in the crook of the Monegasque’s neck. “Please don’t drag me into this mess. It’s never good when you try to meddle.”

“Oscar will not tell him the truth and they’re both dancing around each other like school children! They need a bit of meddling now.”

“Are you sure it won’t make things worse?”

“Trust me, mon amour, you will like this a lot! It’s just the perfect thing to get things moving along.”

Max leaned forward, giving him a soft chaste kiss before parting, smirking as Charles had quickly chased after his lips. “You are such an evil mastermind. Go on then, go down your thing.” 

While Max and Charles were busy conducting a plan,  Lando was hopelessly dancing with Daniel who was trying his best to look interested. Lando let himself immerse with the music, feeling as though the music was being pumped into his veins. Daniel grabbed Lando by the hip, pulling them together and put his best effort into dancing with the wild boy. While he was in his daze of dancing, he hadn't noticed that the boy he once gazed at was now watching them from afar. 

The brunette girl in front of Oscar whose name he couldn’t even remember (Jenny- Jane- Janet?) suddenly surged up to close the gap between them, grabbing his face and pulling them together. Oscar panicked and threw his head back, his hands closed around hers and yanked them away from his face. 

He gave her a quick look of surprise as she looked at him with a confused expression. “What are you doing?” Oscar sputtered out, god he was too sober for anything like this. His eyes kept glancing back over to Lando dancing with Daniel, feeling a pang of hurt spreading in his chest. Oscar dropped the girl's hands and took a step back. 

“I was going to kiss you?” She responded like it was the most obvious thing in the world. And it probably was. But Oscar had been so focused on his best friend that he didn’t even realise where the girl thought this was going. “Considering we’ve been dancing together, I thought we could... You know, take this further?” She said with a suggestive tone, running her finger across Oscar’s chest as she tried to close the gap between them again. 

Looking back, he wasn’t even too sure why he agreed to dance with her. She approached him and Oscar just said yes without thinking about it, plus it was easier to say yes and get it over with. He wasn’t used to the attention and Lando had disappeared to the bar for god knows how long. He wanted to clear his mind, to clear his body of the effects from the intoxicating dance he had with Lando, of how the older boy looked heavenly to him as he impressed the environment around him.

But now- now he was having to watch Lando dance with Daniel the way he danced for Oscar, making him feel truly sick to his core . 

“Look, you seem nice, Janet, but I’m not really interested.There’s a lot of other people here tonight who’d be very lucky to kiss you. I’m gonna shoot off and find my friend. Hope you enjoy the rest of your night.” Oscar quickly uttered before making a beeline for Lando, his eyes set on the swaying boy.

“You’re an asshole! My name is Laura!” She shouted and he winced as he sped up to get away as fast as possible. 

Okay, not his finest moment. 

With a steady determination, Oscar advanced towards the dancing pair of Daniel and Lando. A quiet storm was building beneath the surface of his skin as his eyes landed on Daniel, who was already looking at him with a guilty expression. Protectiveness surged through his body, the air cracking with tension as they stared at each other. Oscar really liked Daniel, both being Australians, he was very funny and all-in-all a good person, but it did nothing to soothe the distaste for the Aussie that he felt in that moment.

Daniel, acknowledging the tension in the air, stepped away from the scene and mouthed a subtle ‘sorry’ over Lando’s shoulder. He didn’t bother waiting for Oscar’s response, leaving before anything further could happen.

Oscar found himself there standing idly as he observed Lando moving graciously, as if he were the only thing in the world. He extended his arm and gently tapped Lando’s shoulder, calling his name loudly. 

Lando opened his eyes slowly with a subtle smile, likely expecting to be met with Daniel’s face. However, his expression morphed into a grin as he realised it was Oscar standing before him. “Oh, you’ve finally decided to join me?” He remarked with a touch of playful sarcasm. 

“Why were you dancing with Daniel?” Oscar deadpanned, keeping his voice stoic, attempting to not reveal any hint of emotion that wants to burst from him. 

“Can’t I dance with a friend?” Lando asked innocently, adding a charm to his voice as he spoke and fluttering his eyelashes. 

Oscar scoffed in disbelief and rolled his eyes. “You were definitely not dancing like he was a friend, Lando.”

“No? You were dancing with me like that earlier, Oscar. Was that not just two friends dancing?” At that, Oscar stayed silent and didn’t make any comments. Instead, he ran his tongue along his bottom lip and bit into it. Lando felt giddy seeing Oscar so wound up, taking little steps forward, grinning when Oscar didn’t move a muscle. “Why does it matter how I was dancing, anyway? Are you jealous?” 

Honestly? Fuck Lando and his unwavering confidence when he drinks. 

“Just-” Oscar abruptly cut himself off with a frustrated groan, running a hand through his hair stressfully. “I don’t understand why you’re dancing with him like that! I thought you weren’t into Daniel like that.” His voice mixed with confusion and concern.

“I’m not!” Lando protested, the slurring was less evident as his voice raised, feeling like the world around him was becoming clearer the deeper into arguing they got. “Daniel’s my friend and so are you, I can dance with whoever I want and however I want.” He asserted a firmness in his words. 

Lando honestly wasn’t sure what he’d expected when he asked Daniel to dance with him. Sure, he wanted to get a reaction out of Oscar and he wanted the younger one to just notice him, but he never expected the green-eyed insecurity to come with it. He never expected Oscar to be looking at him like his breath was taken away and arguing over how he was dancing. 

“I’m not saying you can’t!” Oscar exclaimed. 

“Then what are you saying?” Lando challenged him, stepping up into his personal space. The world moved around them, strobe lights flashing and everyone singing around them slowed as if time stopped for them. 

“Lando, I-” His voice trailed off, staring into the furious eyes of Lando. Oscar’s breath caught in his throat, rendering him speechless as he struggled to morph the thoughts swirling in his head into words.

“I saw you dancing with that girl, you know.” Lando blurted accusingly, his accusation hanging in the air between them. The Aussie’s expression had softened and held a look of conflict as they stood there locked within their gaze. “Why were you dancing with her?” The hurt was evident in his voice as he questioned. 

“I don’t know!” Oscar admitted exasperatedly and threw his hands in the air. “She asked me and I just didn’t really think about it. I wish I hadn’t done it and I wish I just waited for you. It wasn’t the same as it was with you.”

Lando stayed quiet for a few moments, trying his best to take the words in while his drunken brain felt like it was flying all around his head. “Why is it okay for you to dance with someone but not me?”

“Lando, I’m sorry, that’s really not what I meant and I shouldn’t have made this into an issue.” Oscar apologised and took Lando’s hand into his own, giving it three squeezes. Lando felt a hitch in his breathing as the three squeezes came, he returned them instantly. “I’m honestly not even sure how I can tell you this or even begin to explain it. You truly mean so much to me and I hated seeing you with Daniel then.” Lando looked at him with his mouth slightly parted, a look of wonder in his eyes. “I don’t even know what came over me, but I knew I just needed to come over here and stop it. For weeks I’ve to watch you put yourself in danger and I’ve literally gone out of my fucking mind worrying that something was going to happen to you, I can’t keep this from you anymore. I’m not good at expressing my feelings or even talking in general, you know that…  What I’m trying to say is, Lando, that I really-”

“Please can Lando and Oscar make their way to the DJ booth to sing a little duet for us!”

Oscar groaned as he was interrupted, rubbing his face in frustration with his free hand. Lando shook his head as he pulled himself out of a lovesick trance from the depths of Oscar’s eyes. “The world is truly out to get me at this point.” Oscar said and reluctantly made his way towards the DJ booth, Lando allowing himself to be dragged like a lost lamb.

“Oscar, fuck the karaoke. What were you going to say to me?” Lando pleaded desperately, his heart screaming at him to get on his knees and beg for the answer, if that’s what it takes. If it was truly what he thought it was, then it couldn’t wait any longer. “Please- talk to me, fuck everyone else. It’s me and you, forever and always, right? Just tell me.” His brain kept spewing out words, rambling as they made their way to the DJ booth. 

“It’s okay, Lando.” Oscar looked over his shoulder, flashing a soft smile that just made Lando’s stomach flip. “We’ll do this song and then we’ll have a talk, okay?”

“The world can wait for us!” Lando begged, trying to tug Oscar to a standstill. It was no use, the Aussie was a lot stronger than him and was determined to get to the DJ booth now.

“You’re right and we really need to talk, I think we’ve both got a lot of things we want to get off our chests and I can’t wait to tell you everything, Lando.” Oscar’s eyes sparkled brightly at him. “But we’ve got all the time in the world, we may as well have some fun before we get into… that.” Lando nodded slowly, accepting that fate was once again working overtime to stop them.

He let it go for now, he let himself enjoy the fact that Oscar Piastri is somehow going to be singing in front of a room full of people instead. “I can’t believe you’re actually agreeing to do karaoke. Who are you and what have you done with my Oscar?” Lando giggled to himself. 

“Well, I’m assuming you signed us up for it, I’ll always do it if it’s what you want.”

“While I appreciate the sentiment, I didn’t actually sign us up this time.” Lando admitted sheepishly. “Although- I’m not totally against the idea of it.” 

“Right, of course you didn’t sign us up for it.” Oscar sighed heavily. 

They reached the DJ booth quickly and were ushered to by the surrounding people to stand in front of it. The DJ reached down, handing them two microphones. “Mate, mind telling us who signed us up?” Lando questioned with a raised eyebrow. He tossed the microphone between his two hands, however his co-ordination was still off and he nearly dropped it. 

The DJ pointed somewhere out in the crowd. “Pretty sure it was the guy who organised this thing, brown hair, was dragging a blonde guy around.” Lando scrowled to himself upon realising exactly who it could’ve been. “You guys are singing ‘Love Story’, get ready, it’s about to start!”

Love Story?

The first notes of the song started and Lando instantly recognised it as the song by Taylor Swift. He wasn’t a big Swiftie, but who didn’t know this song? Oscar apparently, since he had a panicked look on his face. Lando looked at the screen with the lyrics and glanced back at Oscar next to him. “We’ll sing the whole thing together. Are you ready to follow my lead, Piastri?” Lando smirked, referring back to their earlier conversation. 

Oscar’s face lit up, instantly recognising and reliving that moment where Lando said that phrase from earlier in the night. “When will I ever not follow your lead?” The two laughed to themselves before looking back at the lyrics on the screen.

“Get ready for the best duet of the night, ladies and gentlemen! Because we are about to blow your minds!” Lando shouted into the microphone, causing Oscar to cringe with second-hand embarrassment. 

The lyrics soon came in and Lando was practically yelling the lyrics into the microphone, overshadowing Oscar’s terrible out-of-pitch singing as he had no clue what the lyrics to the verses were. The Aussie hesitated as he sang the lyrics before launching himself into the chorus, following Lando’s lead as per usual. 

As the song picked up pace and the two’s enthusiasm heightened, their friends had managed to push their way to the front of the crowd and were screaming the lyrics right back at them, all jumping around in excitement. Oscar’s sweet laugh echoed through the microphone as he saw his friends jumping in the air and singing with them. As usual, Lando belted out the one bit that everyone knows. As his confidence blossomed, he fully let himself go for it. “ It’s a love story, baby, just say yes.” 

Neither of them were singers by any means, but they revelled in the moment and Oscar quickly loosened up the more Lando went for it. He may have purposely made himself look a bit more like a fool to encourage Oscar to relax a little bit, being the good extroverted best friend he was.

“This love is difficult, but it’s real.” Lando felt butterflies in his stomach as the two sang the lyric in sync, their hands briefly brushing against each other as he jolted away at the touch. His hand felt like it was burning, electricity pooling in him, the words and the touch coming too close to his heart.

Their friends had gotten their phones out now, recording the scene in front of them. It wasn’t often you had the Oscar Piastri singing karaoke in a room full of people, they had to cherish the moment while they still could- he knew it would be used as blackmail later on.

“I got tired of waiting, wondering if you were ever coming around.” Lando approached Oscar, throwing his arm around him, causing him to be slightly unbalanced due to the height difference. Oscar looked at him, no longer singing and was enchanted by the boy holding onto him. “My faith in you was fading.” The Aussie gulped nervously, feeling a weakness in his knees as it truly felt like Lando was singing the lyrics to him.

His gaze fixated on the delicate but sharp features of Lando’s face, captivated by the glow coming from him that seemed to brighten every room he was in. Lando allowed himself to be sucked into the passion of the song, closing his eyes as he held the microphone like his life depending on him singing this song. Oscar felt a tingle throughout his body as he watched him closely, cursing to himself once he realised the true power that Lando held over him. 

Oscar’s eyes glowed with an unmistakable tenderness, unaware of the fact that their friends were currently watching his ‘heart-eyes-Piastri’ moment intently, one of them even getting a sneaky picture of it. His gaze spoke a thousand words, admiration pouring out of him as his smile grew the more he watched. In that moment, amidst the laughter and music, the focal point of his gaze was just Lando, always Lando. 

“Marry me, Juliet, you’ll never have to be alone! I love you, and that’s all I really know!” Lando belted and started jumping up and down, bumping into Oscar and pulling him out of his trance-like state. Oscar looked around and quickly began singing again before Lando could notice. He felt a pink tint growing on his face once he saw the smirking face of Charles staring at him with his phone’s flashlight on. ‘ Fuck, of course he was gathering evidence.’ He gulped nervously as the thought of them all knowing and watching lingered around.

He decided to say ‘fuck it’ , they all already knew, Oscar may as well embarrass himself with Lando

Oscar let himself indulge in the music and , feeling a gush of adoration run through him when Lando flashed him a wide smile. The rest of the party went into a frenzy as the lyrics were belted from all corners of the room. Lando felt his heart swell with an outpouring of affection, coursing through his veins as he directed the lyrics to the Australian next to him. 

Their gaze locked, an invisible force holding them captive as they couldn’t tear their eyes away. “It’s a love story, baby, just say-” Lando began to sing, his demeanour calming down.  

“-Yes.” Oscar finished, whispering it into the microphone as their jumping came to a halt.

The room erupted into cheers and claps, as well as the occasional wolf whistle from who Lando could only deduce was Daniel. However, the only important thing in the room to Lando right now was Oscar. 

Oscar, who was looking at him with those goddamn eyes and flickering his gaze downwards before filtering back up to his eyes. Oscar, who he had felt staring at him while he was singing. Oscar, who he’d been in love with for the past two years. Oscar, who had always been there for him, even after all his incidents with Spiderman. Oscar, who would listen to him ramble for days. Oscar, who was leaning towards him-

Wait. Oscar was leaning towards him? 

Lando found himself unconsciously leaning forward to Oscar, drawing them both closer and closer like a magnetic force pulling them together. The Brit’s eyes flickered down to Oscar’s lips, enticing him to just finally take the plunge. Lando’s chest was rising up and down rapidly, the breaths coming out staggered. He could do this, Lando rushed forward and raised his hand cup Oscar’s cheek-

“Dudes, that was amazing!” Logan came barreling forward and Lando practically growled to himself, cursing the American for interrupting the moment. It was time to go back to hating him, Lando presumed. He dropped his hand and Oscar blinked himself back into existence, shooting Logan a weak smile. 

The Brit looked at the crowd, most people were now back to dancing while their friends were still watching them, who all stood there either looking sheepish or annoyed at Logan. Arthur had been standing with his arm out, presumably to try and stop Logan from approaching them. Arthur shot him a sad look, Lando looked away to glare at Logan again. 

“For fucks sake, Logan, really?” Alex groaned. 

“Yeah, way to fucking ruin it!” Charles exclaimed and tried to stomp forward, only stopped by Max’s arm wrapped around his middle. 

“What have I done?” Logan asked with genuine confusion. He looked at them all before turning to Lando and Oscar, who both were glaring at him like he committed an atrocious crime. “Oh.” He said as embarrassment instantly took over him. 

“You know what? I’ll be back in a few, I’m just popping to the bathroom.” Lando excused himself and didn’t bother waiting for a response from them, he couldn’t bear to look Oscar in the eyes right after that. The moment was truly ruined, all thanks to Logan.

He heard someone briefly call after him, but he was too busy speed walking towards the toilets at the back, abandoning the scene. Lando threw open the bathroom door and stumbled towards the sink, crashing into it. His hands quickly gripped onto the edge of the sink as shaky and shallow breaths escaped him. 

Lando honestly had no clue what was happening between them tonight and he couldn’t even begin to put into words what his heart and mind were screaming at him.  All the dancing with Oscar and Daniel, all the drinks he was handed and the shots he took at the bar, all the flirting, the arguing, the singing.

It was just all too much for him, all too overwhelming. 

Lando reached forward to turn the cold tap on at the sink, using his hands to cup the water once he felt his body was stable enough to stand and delicately rinsed his face. As the water washed over his skin, he relished in the refreshing coldness against his burning hot cheeks. The only sound that filled the bathroom was the running of the tap and Lando’s occasional sharp intakes. 

He took a long look at himself in the large mirror situated over the top of the sinks. His face was flushed, although the redness was fading away, and his hair was a curly mess after running his hand through it so much. Lando huffed and began to fix his hair, adjusting his shirt, fixing his necklace. If he was going back out there, he was coming out looking like a new man. 

Suddenly, the bathroom door flung open again and Lando ducked his head back down to the sink, pretending to wash his hands as the newcomer entered. He grimaced in embarrassment at nearly being caught checking himself out in the mirror. 

“Do not stop for me, Lando. You got to look your best tonight!” The man spoke in a thick accent. 

Lando turned to the man speaking to him and saw the younger of the Monegasque brothers talking to him, Arthur. He closed the bathroom door behind him and approached Lando by the sink, pulling himself up on the counter and taking a sip of his drink. They looked at each other for a few seconds before Lando raised his eyebrow. “What are you doing, mate?”

Arthur shrugged his shoulders and popped his mouth off of the colourful straw. “I could ask you the same thing, why are you here fixing your hair when Oscar is out there looking like a love-sick puppy?”

Lando groaned and went back to fixing his hair in the mirror, anything to distract him from having to talk about it with Arthur. “It’s complicated.”

“I’m all about complicated, try me.” A smirk played on the younger boy’s face. 

“You wouldn’t know what complicated is even if it hit you in the face like a brick, Arthur.”

The Monegasque rolled his eyes and gently placed his drink down next to him. “Do you forget who my brother is? I’ve had to watch him for years complain about Max only to end up with him.”

 “... True.”

“And that I like Logan but I live in Monaco?” Arthur tilted his head at him, like he knew he already won. 

A long pause filled the air before he finally spoke up. “Okay, okay,” Lando admitted defeat, not in the mood for one of Arthur’s mind tricks. “I get it. You’ve made your point, what do you really want? You clearly came here to say something.”

Arthur hummed with a snicker and turned his body slightly so he was facing Lando, his body moving like a sly panther. “You had me fooled up until that moment there, I thought you and Oscar were together.”

Lando chuckled quietly and shook his head at himself in the mirror.“You thought we were together?” He asked, flabbergasted.

“Oui, you look at each other like an old married couple.”

The Brit let out a loud laugh as he imagined him and Oscar as an old married couple, sitting on a porch bickering over the little things in life. His laughter died down and a bittersweet smile formed, he looked down at his hands, unsure what to do with himself. “That’s just me and Oscar for you, I guess. How long have you thought we were together?”

Arthur tapped his chin thoughtfully. “I’d say probably since the last time I visited, so since this the summer just gone. You would not believe my shock when I saw you both dancing with other people. What happened there, mate?” 

“Honestly, Arthur, you don’t want me to start. Once I get into it, there’s no turning back, I’m a talker.”

“Like I said, try me.”

And so Lando talked for what felt like hours. He talked about it all from the beginning, from the moment he met Oscar, all the little things about him that made Lando adore him like the way he’d counteract everything Lando had to say or the way he’d scrunched his nose cutely will laughing at a joke that clearly wasn’t funny. He spoke about the weird behaviours like his morning, afternoon and night runs, his constant overworking at the lab and always being called in, the lies he’d uncovered about his old library job. He talked about how things seemed to take a drastic change, both showing jealousy, the thing Oscar was just about to say before they were rudely interrupted, their little moment before Logan also rudely interrupted them. Somehow, he started ranting about Spiderman in the midst of it all while trying to explain to Arthur what happened at Winter Wonderland, how it changed the dynamic with him and Oscar. 

His brain was working overtime as he tried to remember everything, spilling his guts and laying himself bare for Arthur. The whole time, the Monegasque nodded along, listening intently, his brows furrowing when he’d mention a thing or two. 

“-And well, that’s everything up until now. Like you’ve seen tonight, it’s all just so fucking weird and I don’t know where I stand with him. I don’t know what to do.” Lando slouched over the sink, burying his head in his hands. 

“I think you’re a fool.” Arthur deadpanned.

“What?” Lando exclaimed, turning to him in shock. “I pour my heart out in front of you and that’s all you have to say to me?”

“Idiot.” Arthur muttered and jumped down from the sink counter, pulled Lando by his shoulders so they were facing each other. “Everything Oscar does is for you. His whole livelihood is you, he’s obsessed with you. But you know that really, you know he loves you.”

“I- I don’t know anything for sure, but could he have feelings for me? Maybe…” Lando could be stupid at times, but even he acknowledged that he and Oscar had been dancing around each other for weeks now, feelings and tension bubbling to the surface, ready to implode any moment from now. “I know how he looks at me, everyone’s been telling me for years. And there’s been so many times now where something could’ve happened- I just don't know what to do.”

“How is it not obvious already? You need to tell him how you feel!” Arthur exclaimed and shook him by the shoulders. 

“And what if I’m wrong? What if he doesn’t feel the same, Arthur? Don’t forget, he’s been lying to me for the past year and god knows what else he’s lying about, do I really want to be with someone like that?”

“Yes! For the love of god, yes!” Arthur shouted and released Lando, flailing his arms around as he spoke. “You two- you are both insufferable, you’re even as bad as my brother-” Lando gasped offendedly. “-you do not know why he lied, you don’t know what else there is, sure. But all you need to do is ask him. You mentioned that Oscar said he had a lot to tell you, oui? So go find him, go tell him you love him and make sure he tells you everything.”

“Just- just tell him?” Lando uttered to himself quietly. 

“A blind person could even see the love radiating from you, Lando, it’s actually a bit sickening. Go find him, tell him how you feel, and get the truth.” Arthur said, walking behind Lando and pushing him towards the bathroom door. 

“Yeah…” Lando nodded along, his confidence making a quick return as he allowed himself to be pushed to the bathroom door. “You’re right- by the end of tonight, I’m gonna make Oscar Piastri my boyfriend!”

“Yeah you are!” Arthur cheered for him.  

“I can’t wait to tell him!” Lando exclaimed with visible excitement.

“You don’t have to wait.” Arthur turned him around just before they reached the door so they were facing each other again. “Own the moment, Lando.” He gave him a poorly done wink, just like his brother, but it was enough reassurance for Lando to get himself out there and do what needs to be done. 

Arthur pushed him out of the bathroom door, enveloping him in the sea of strobe lights. His eyes adjusted to the dark to the lights again and he didn’t waste a second before running off to find Oscar. As Lando ran back to the dance floor, he tripped over his own feet slightly, but nothing was enough to stop him now. 

Oscar said they’d talk after the song, he intended to hold him to it. 

Lando pushed his way through the crowd, muttering a half-assed sorry to everyone as he barged through. He managed to get back to the DJ booth, sighing in relief when he saw his friends still crowding around the area. He approached them quickly, a frown beginning to form as he noticed that Oscar was missing from them all. 

Again.

Pierre spotted him immediately, a hesitant look on his face as Lando drew closer. The rest of them turned to look at him now with apologetic expressions. “Why do you all look like someone’s died?” Lando’s joke hung in the air, hanging dry as they all continued to look like they were sorry for him. “Where’s Oscar?” Lando scanned the crowd again, begging to himself that the Aussie wasn’t off dancing with someone else again. 

“Oscar…” Daniel began to say, but Max was quick to appear in front of him, taking over. 

“Listen- I need you to stay calm, okay?” Max asked him. 

Lando furrowed his brows in confusion. He suddenly felt really small under all their gazes, feeling their pity like a thick cloud of smoke in the air. “What’s going on?” Lando asked with uncertainty. 

Max sighed and looked over his shoulder at the rest of their friends, who nodded at him. He turned back to face Lando with a wince. “Oscar’s left, he told us he needed to go. He didn’t bother explaining anything before you ask, he just told us to tell you he’s sorry.”

And then, Lando felt like everything around him shattered into a million pieces, being quickly reminded why exactly he and Oscar would never work. There were too many secrets, too many lies and broken promises for him to ever build any type of relationship from the ground up. He felt like he was pulled back into reality, out of his fantasy, and then came the anger. 

“He’s sorry?” Lando spat and pushed Max’s arm out of the way as he tried to reach for the younger boy. “I asked him to do one fucking thing for me and he can’t even do that.” 

Daniel rubbed at his face with stress. “Lando-” He began, but again was interrupted. 

“He’s only just left a minute ago. Lando, if you leave now, you can still catch him.” Logan quickly said, jumping out from the circle of his friends. “Go, now.” The American urged. 

Lando muttered a quick thank you and flew through the crowd with ease, navigating his way through like his life depended on it. As he was pushing through, he saw a mess of brown wavy hair walking out of the exit and he gritted his teeth. His anger carried him through the venue until he was outside the building. 

In the veil of darkness, only illuminated by the neon lights filtering out of the windows, rain descended upon him. Lando cursed himself for not having his coat on hand, but he had more important things to worry about. He could see Oscar stood there further up the pavement, scrutinising the area and looking like he was preparing for something. 

“Oscar!” Lando shouted as he stomped across the pavement, bringing himself closer and closer. He saw Oscar’s body stiffen, knowing exactly who was after him. The Aussie spun on his feet slowly, a look of fear evident on him as an angry Lando came flying at him. “Where the fuck are you going?” 

“Lando, I’m sorry.” Oscar began to say, shaking his head to move his wet hair out of his eyes. “I’m so sorry, we’ll have our talk soon, okay?”

“No, you don’t get to do this now, you don’t get to start apologising and still leave . For years, I’ve let it all go, I’ve let you do what you needed to do. You cancel plans with all our friends, if you agree to come out you’ll leave without anyone knowing where you went, you’re gone every night and every morning. You claim you’re on your runs but I know it’s not true, not anymore. You’ve made me look like a fool for the last two years, keeping up your lies and never telling me anything that’s going on. When were you going to tell me you quit your job, Oscar? I tried to call in sick for you at the library and I had to find out from them that you actually quit your job last year!” Lando shouted, all the pent up anger he’d been feeling suddenly rushing out of him like a river with a broken dam.

Oscar stood there gobsmacked, his mouth opening and closing like a blubbering fish. He was stunned and Lando felt an odd satisfaction in knowing he rendered the Aussie speechless. “I’ve never bothered you about any of it, I’ve never questioned it because if you wanted to talk to me, you would. I thought by giving you the space to do what you want, I could live with it, but I’m constantly kept in the dark about everything when I thought we were supposed to be like a team. I was always willing to wait for you, but I can’t do it- not anymore, Oscar.” 

“Lando, I’m so sorry but I can’t stay. I need to go.” Oscar muttered with a guilty tone.  

“Why? Why do you need to go so desperately?” Lando pleaded with him, his anger leaving him like it was washed away by the rain. All he could feel was an empty void in his chest, a deep sadness gnawing at its cage. 

“I just can’t, Lando. Not right now- I told you, we can talk later. I promise you, I’ll tell you everything. You deserve to know everything, but I can’t do this right now.” Oscar tried to begin explaining.  

“No! I’m done with your shitty excuses for promises, all you do is break them!” Lando exclaimed as his eyes watered from all of the emotion he was feeling at this moment. 

“Lando-”  

“You promised you’d stay, just this one time, and you can’t even do that.” Lando said exasperatedly, gasping to himself quietly when he felt the trickle of a tear fall down his face. 

The tears mixed in with the rain, but Oscar’s eyes followed the tear as it descended, as if he knew that they had started to fall. “And that was a mistake, I shouldn’t have promised it when I knew I probably couldn’t stay.” He spoke with a heavy heart. 

“Please stay, Oscar. I’m begging you, please just stay this once. We need to talk.” Oscar looked away from Lando and stared out into the open street, empty as they stood their arguing.. “Oscar… if you go now, I won’t be there at the flat waiting for you.” Lando stared at him with determination, giving him an ultimatum. 

“Lando, please…” Oscar pleaded with desperation etched into his features, his hand reaching out for Lando’s own. He took a few steps back, out of the Aussie’s grasp. The younger boy’s face fell, his eyes looking distraught. 

He stood firm, resolute with his decision. “Make your choice, Oscar. You can either stay here or tell me the truth, and I’ll accept it. Or, you can leave now and lose me. Either way, I’m not trying anymore.” The words hung in the air between them, the tension so thick it could be cut with a knife. 

“It’s not that simple, Lando.” Oscar whispered as his eyes watered.

“It never is with you.” Lando remarked with his voice laced with sorrow. 

And the decision was made. Oscar turned around and ran off before Lando could get another word in edgewise. The rainwater splashed up as his trainers hit the ground running, leaving behind his trail. Lando stood there dumbfounded and with disbelief that Oscar truly made the decision to leave him. 

The sky wept in sync with Lando as his quiet tears poured from his red eyes, the patter of the rain falling creating its own sombre melody for Lando as he accepted his fate. He and Oscar were not what fate wanted, always finding a way to tear them apart before they could have anything between them. It broke his heart when it dawned on him. The rain mixed with his tears as he allowed himself to be soaked to the bone. 

So much for making Oscar Piastri his boyfriend tonight. 

He was so caught up in looking into the distance, praying to himself that Oscar would come running back, apologising and explaining, that he hadn’t noticed Charles had appeared to the side of him. Lando felt a thick fabric wrapped around his shoulders and he jolted in shock, meeting the sorrowful gaze of the older Leclerc brother. His chest was rising up and down and he was breathing heavily, likely running after the two boys once he caught wind of Oscar leaving. 

“C-Charles?” Lando’s voice cracked and he sniffled loudly. He could imagine that he looked like a poor sight to see right now. His silk-like shirt turned even darker as the wet fabric clung to his body, his eyes would be puffy and his once curly hair would be a flat mess against his head. 

“You don’t need to say anything, Max and I are taking you to my place.” Charles reassured him and moved him until they were walking back towards the venue. “Pierre hasn’t drank tonight, come on, he’ll take us home.”

“No- you need to stay, it’s your party!” Lando weakly protested, frowning to himself deeply once he saw Max and Pierre exiting the building, heading to the park car out front. “I’ll go by myself, promise.”

Charles shrugged. “I’ll come with you, put you to bed, and then if I feel like it I’ll come back for the party. Besides, I got what I wanted tonight, anyway.” Charles smiled at him reassuringly. 

Time seemed to pass in a blur, their voices becoming nothing but an irritating ringing in his ear. He found himself being gently guided into the back of the car with Charles sitting next to him. Max and Pierre climbed into the front seats, the two glancing over at Lando before turning the engine on and driving off. 

Charles moved Lando’s frail body, laying Lando’s head down in his lap and his eyes fluttered shut, suddenly feeling exhaustion overwhelming him. He curled up on the seat and nestled his head into Charles’ legs, the Monegasque running a hand through Lando’s curls softly. 

Lando hummed, feeling sleep starting to overtake him. “You’re okay, everything is going to be okay. We’ll fix it.” It was the last thing he heard before he succumbed to the darkness dragging him. 

Chapter End Notes
