“What use is it being Spider-Man’s boyfriend if I can’t get away doing risky shit?” Lando complained, taking tentative steps back. He bumped into Oscar’s chest, letting out a soft sound as he felt the large hands resting on his hips to steady him. 

“It’ll be your funeral if you fall off it again, Lando.” Oscar said. “Sometimes I think you do these things on purpose just for me to save you.”

“Maybe I just wanna feel my boyfriend’s muscles after he saves me!” Lando shot back jokingly, his arms reaching back in an awkward attempt to squeeze Oscar’s muscular frame. 

“You don’t have to go throwing yourself off roofs to feel me up!” Oscar responded exasperatedly, watching as Lando glanced over his shoulder at him with a cheeky smile. “We can sit there together, but I’m keeping a tight grip on you.” He commanded.

“I’m not complaining.” Lando grumbled to himself, shrugging. 

Oscar gently guided them to the edge of the roof, easing Lando down as he settled on the concrete himself, legs swinging freely in the air. True to his word, his arm remained wrapped around the Brit as they sat there, the older boy leaning into his touch. 

Beneath their dangling legs, the city was alive with the nightlife and celebrations of the people of London, all waiting for the countdown. The streets were still adorned with Christmas lights, illuminating the scenario, it would almost distract you from the drunken crowds and laughter of the people below them. 

“Do you ever wonder what our lives would be like if one thing went differently?” Lando interrupted the comfortable silence between them, his head tilting up as he watched Oscar’s face for a reaction. 

“What do you mean by that?” Oscar gazed at him curiously.

“I mean- imagine if we went back to a few years ago and one thing in that timeline changed, we wouldn’t be here now. If we hadn’t lived in the same accommodation then, we’d have never met. We didn’t have any mutual friends before knowing each other and we weren’t in the same classes.” Lando explained thoughtfully. “And what if I didn’t fall off the roof in the first year? Spiderman wouldn’t have saved me, I wouldn’t have idolised him-well, you. Maybe we wouldn’t have pushed ourselves in this direction.”

“What are you trying to say?” Oscar asked with a gentle tone. Lando had a tendency to ramble and become lost in his own thoughts, and Oscar was fluent in the language of Lando. “We’re together now, that’s what matters.” 

“I’m saying, I’m scared that this is the only ending we could’ve had. What if every other path led to us not being together?” Lando said, his teeth chewing on his bottom lip as his eyes filled with worry. “I hate the thought that maybe we shouldn’t have ended up together because every other alternative would mean we wouldn’t have given it a chance.”

“Everything happens for a reason, I believe fate wanted it to happen this way.” Oscar reassured him. “In every alternative reality, in every possible ending of our story, I’ll always love you in each and every single one.”

Lando’s face grew a radiant smile, reaching up to plant a chaste kiss on Oscar’s winter bitten cheek. They gazed lovingly into each other’s eyes, unwilling to let go of the emotions flowing between them, wanting to live in this moment forever. 

“The countdown is about to start!” Charles shouted, the two of them glancing over their shoulders to see their friends gathering in a circle together. 

“Should we go back over there?” Oscar asked. 

“No.” Lando shook his head quickly. “We’ll stay here, I think we deserve this moment to ourselves.”

“10!”

“9!”

“8!”

“7!”

“6!”

“I love you, Oscar. It’s you and me, forever and always.” Lando professed, his hands wrapped in Oscar’s coat as he pulled their faces closer together. 

“5!”

“4!”

“I love you too, Lando, and I always will. In every ending, you’re mine.” His hand traced Lando’s cold cheek, their faces just mere seconds from meeting. The cold mist of their breaths mixed together, excitement bubbling to the surface as they eagerly awaited for the countdown to end.

“3!”

“2!”

“1!”

“Happy New Year!” Everyone shouted in unison, the sound of everyone clinking their drinks together sounded and was soon cut off by their shared kisses. 

Lando seized the moment with any delay, pressing his lips against Oscar’s. Their surroundings melted away into nothingness, leaving only the sweet warmth and love that enveloped them. In that moment as their bodies overlooked the city, the world was privy to their passionate display. 

The night sky erupted with bright and colourful fireworks all the way from the London Eye, each burst painting it like a canvas made specially for them. The booms of the fireworks echoed the beat of Lando’s own heart, loud and overwhelming in his own chest. 

“I can never get enough of you.” Oscar whispered against Lando’s red, glistening lips. 

Lando smirked within the kiss, his entire being set alight with want and passion. “I’m always willing to give a little more.” 

*

Lando awoke to a clattering noise coming from inside his flat, letting out a groan as he turned over in the bed. His arm reached out for Oscar instinctively, falling flat against the mattress. Eyes opening slowly, Lando was met with the empty space next to him. He tried to ignore the disappointment swelling in his chest as he realised Oscar must’ve had some Spiderman responsibilities to attend to. 

He sat up in the bed as he remembered the loud noise that sounded a mere minute ago, body relaxing as he heard his boyfriend cursing from inside his own bedroom. Oscar must’ve left and returned home through his own window, always mindful of the fact that Lando was still sleeping. 

Several months had passed since they made things official and Lando discovered Oscar’s free time was spent swinging around the city as Spiderman. It was taking some getting used to, dealing with the anxiety blooming in his chest every time he heard the notification on Oscar’s phone, signalling he’d be living within the next two minutes. It also took some time to get used to Oscar now proudly showing off his abilities, no longer restraining himself as he used his webs and his strength for everything. Waking up alone sometimes also wasn’t so ideal, but Oscar always did his best to try and come home before Lando awoke, crawling back into his dedicated space in the bed. 

Shaking off the grogginess and remains of his sleep, he rose from the bed, deciding he may as well make some tea now that he was wide awake. Making his way out of his room, he shuffled his way over to Oscar’s own, pushing open the now unbroken door (which he was still grateful for, having celebrated at the time for the return of their deposit). As he entered the room. Lando was about to ask him if wanted anything to drink, but his jaw fell flat as he saw the sight in front of him. 

One thing he’d probably never get used to was walking into Oscar’s room to find him with his suit torn in multiple places, laced with blood. 

Oscar collapsed on his bed, pressing down hard on a wound and his eyes trailed over to the open door, panic rising in his eyes. “L-Lando…” He breathed heavily, his chest wheezing slightly with the rise and fall. The hero pulled his mask off, his brown hair wrecked with sweat and revealing a purple bruise on his eye, a little swollen. His lip was slightly busted, blood smeared around the edges. 

“Oscar, what happened?” Lando whispered to himself, filled with sorrow. Before he could register it, his body moved until he was by the side of the bed, kneeling to the side as he tried to examine the injuries on Oscar’s body further. There were cuts and bruises littered across his body, seen through the torn fabric, nothing he couldn’t handle, it would heal throughout the night. But the wound on his stomach that was off covered by Oscar’s hand? That was concerning, that one was still bleeding profusely.

“Kraven… Kraven the Hunter, got a notification saying he was going after some people, needed… needed to try and stop him.” Oscar hissed between breaths as Lando moved his hand, checking for anything that was still dislodged in the wound. “I- I managed to stop him, for now. He… he got me good though.”

“I can see that.” Lando mumbled, unsure of what to say. He knew what he signed up for becoming the hero’s boyfriend, he knew the dangers the world would consistently throw at Oscar, almost like a punishment for being such a kind-hearted saviour to everyone, for using the powers that were seemingly ‘cursed’ on him. “We’re gonna have to stop the bleeding, Osc.”

“M’fine… It’ll heal by itself.” Oscar mumbled, swatting his hand away drowsily. He inhaled and exhaled deeply, wincing in pain as he attempted to cover the wound once more. 

Shaking his head, Lando stood and leaned over Oscar. The younger man’s eyes were screwed shut with pain, teeth gritted and head thrown back. Gently placing his hand on Oscar’s cheek, he watched as the hero winced at first, gradually relaxing and nuzzling his face into the touch. “It won’t heal as long as it’s still open like that. We got the first aid kit for a reason, I’ll go grab the stuff.”

“You d-don’t even know what you’re doing with it.” Oscar retorted through a wave of pain as he tried to move. 

“You’re right, I don’t know what I’m doing.” Lando reiterated. “But it’s either I try it, or I’m taking you to the hospital.”

“God, no hospitals, please.” Oscar pleaded with him. “Just- grab your phone, it’ll probably need stitches. There’s an article I found before, had to…” Oscar exhaled loudly. “Had to stitch myself up before, it doesn’t need to be p-perfect, just enough to close the wound.”

Lando nodded, swiftly exiting the room as he looked for the necessary items. Returning promptly, he carried the first aid kit, a bowl of warm water, a cloth and his phone. He made sure to wash his hands before returning, careful not to touch too many surfaces. Clambering onto the bed, he apologised to his boyfriend as Oscar had to move, crying out in pain. Eventually, they managed to shift until Lando was straddling Oscar, allowing him better access to the wound while also keeping him still. 

Oscar told him the name of the article, pulling up a step by step process on cleaning the wound and suturing it. Glancing at the time and date on his phone, Lando sighed quietly. 6th April, 2:03am. Leaning down, much to Oscar’s surprise, he planted a gentle kiss on his lips. “Well, happy birthday, Oscar.” 

“Birthday?” Oscar mumbled as Lando straightened again, taking a pair of gloves out of the first aid box and snapping them on. 

“It’s 2am, baby.” Lando gazed at him lovingly, grabbing the cloth from the side, soaked with the warm water and squeezing out the excess. He began to cleanse the wound as gently as possible, each swipe clearing away the dried and fresh blood along with the dirt surrounding it. Oscar hissed, hands shooting up to grip at Lando’s hips, squeezing them tightly. “Not how I imagined we’d be spending it, but we still have the rest of tomorrow at least.”

“I’m so sorry.” Oscar whispered. 

“Don’t be, you did a good thing today, I’m just glad you came home.”

Once Lando was satisfied with the cleaning, he carefully inspected the wound to check for anything before he continued. As he looked closer, Oscar spoke up. “I think- I think there’s something stuck in there.”

And he was right, Lando could see a small piece of silver metal, shining brightly amongst the wound and blood. “I’m gonna have to use the tweezers to get it out, okay? I’m guessing it’ll hurt, try not to crush my hips.” Lando joked as he mentioned the tight hands holding him, the fingers digging into his skin. Oscar loosened his grip guiltily, his mouth opening to say something before the Brit stopped him. “It’s fine, if it helps you, then I’d rather you do it, Osc.”

Reaching for the sterile wrapped tweezers, Lando tore open the package and set his focus on the wound in front of him. He steadied himself, mindful of the fact he was entering an open wound. God, this wasn’t what he saw himself doing at 2am. He definitely deserved that cup of tea after this. 

As the tweezers were about to enter the wound, Oscar flinched, causing Lando to jump back in fear of hitting something he shouldn’t. “Oscar!”

“I know.” Oscar whined. “My senses could feel it coming and just reacted by themselves. I’ll try and control it better.”

“Try to keep still, baby. That’s probably what’s causing the bleeding and pain.” Lando spoke softly, attempting to ease his nerves as he ran his hand down. Oscar’s arm. 

He nodded drowsily, eyes trailing Lando’s every movement. Lando’s tweezers entered the wound again and by no means was he a perfect surgeon, but he would definitely give himself a pat on the back for this latter. He managed to grab the metal fragment, dislodging it carefully and pulling it out slowly with a controlled motion. Once it exited the wound, Oscar let out a breath while Lando was whooping in victory. 

Twirling the metal in the tweezers, he could see it was quite a sharp piece, a piercing node to it. “It was Kraven’s knife.” Oscar spoke aloud. “Managed to stab me with it, it must’ve broken off.” He said nonchalantly. 

A look of horror was evident on Lando’s face, putting the tweezers and the knife fragment to the side carefully. “Does it feel any better?” Lando questioned, a caring tone to his voice. 

Oscar nodded. “It does. It still hurts, but I can move a little now without feeling like it’s stabbing me all over again.” He laughed to himself

“Oscar…” He said as a warning. 

“Bad timing?”

“Just- I’ve gotta stitch the wound together now.” Lando said quietly. “It might sting a little, I’ve gotta use the antiseptic first.”

“I think my body can fight off any potential infections, Lando.” Oscar tried to protest. 

“And if it doesn’t?” Lando raised his eyebrow challengingly, the hero seemingly backing down. “I’d rather do it and there’s no infection than risk it.”

When the younger didn’t respond, he took that as his sign to start. Lando reached for the antiseptic wipes, gently tearing away the packaging and holding it between his gloved fingers. The sterile scent hung in the air, a satisfying smell to them both. He started swiping against the soon to be stitched skin, the wipe cool against the warm injured skin. Lando also wiped a clean wipe across Oscar’s lip, praying it wouldn’t become infected. Oscar winced as it brushed over certain areas of the wound, but the task was quickly done. 

“You’re doing amazing, baby.” Lando cooed at him, leaning down to give him another kiss as a reward. “I’ll stitch it together and then we’re all done. We’re nearly there.” He gave one last kiss before straightening once more. 

“I can’t believe how careless I was.” Oscar admitted,  a hint of disbelief in his voice.

Lando raised an eyebrow, retrieving the suturing kit and taking out a sterile needle and some thread. “What do you mean? These things happen, Osc. Well- to heroes like you anyway. You weren’t careless. In fact, I’m really proud of you for still fighting him with an injury like this. Very proud.” Lando praised him, admiration for Oscar evident in his words. 

Preparing the needle and thread, he started at the bottom of the wound, piercing the skin and began to stitch the wound delicately. “I just- fuck.” Oscar grimaced in pain. “I’m better than this, I would’ve seen that coming from a mile away usually. He’s strong too- I barely managed to hold him off before the injury. I gave everything into fighting back then, but it’s not enough. I don’t- ow- know what to do. I’m not sure I’m even cut out for this. Mark- he’d be so disappointed in me right now.”

The wound was close to being closed, the grip on his waist tightening and loosening with pain every few seconds. Lando continued working on the wound, by no means was it perfect stitching, but it would do for a wound that would hopefully be mostly healed within a day. 

Lando spoke, his voice calm and steady to ensure the suturing wasn’t affected. “Oscar, it’s okay, you’re far too harsh on yourself. You’ve barely slept recently and you’ve been out most days, you’re exhausted and burning yourself out. You’re not invincible, and that’s okay. I’ll be here to look after you every time, as long as you come home to me alive, we can do it together. For now, let me take care of this, and we’ll talk about what we can do. You’re not alone in this.” 

“Lando-”

“And we both know Mark could never be disappointed in you, he cares for you so much and if you gave him a call now, he’d find every possible way to help you. Do I need to call him?” 

“No.” Oscar shook his head. “You don’t have to. I…” His voice trailed off as he tried to find the words. “Thank you, I love you so much, we’ll figure something out… together.”

“Like I always say, babe. It’s you and me, forever and always. I’ll be by your side as much as I can do and if there is anything I can do, just say the word.”

“I love you.” Oscar said sincerely. 

“I love you too.” Lando completed the final stitches, performing as neat of a closure as he possibly could with his limited skills. He carefully tied off the last knot, taking a pair of scissors to cut off the excess of it. “We’ll need to remove these tomorrow.”

Oscar groaned loudly, his hands dropping from Lando’s hips and hitting his own face. “I forgot about that, just be gentle with me tomorrow.”

“I was gentle today!” Lando scoffed, swinging himself off of Oscar’s lap as he began to tidy up the mess he’d create. He carefully packed away the first aid kit and tore the bloodied gloves off, crumbling up along with the rest of the discarded packaging. “You can do it yourself tomorrow.”

He left the room, putting the supplies away and tipping the slightly tinged red water down the sink, washing the bowl up. After throwing the rubbish away, he returned to the room and was delighted to see Oscar had removed the broken super suit, manoeuvring himself under the covers. 

“We’re sleeping in separate beds then?”

Oscar rolled his eyes. “Come here, you big baby.” He teased, his thick Australian accent sounding as exhaustion was seamlessly taking over him. Lando crawled into the bed, careful to move around his boyfriend as he pulled the blanket over his body. Oscar, ever the gentleman even when injured, pulled Lando closer to him, wrapping his arm around him. “Thank you for taking care of me, Lando. You didn’t have to do that.”

“I’d rather take care of you than wake up to a dead Spidey-Oscar.” Lando said bluntly. 

The room was filled with a tense silence, the weight of what could truly happen to him someday hanging in the air. Being Spiderman was dangerous, constantly facing down various types of villains and criminals, all willing to do whatever it takes for their selfish gains. Oscar was constantly throwing himself in the frontlines, ready to risk his life multiple times a day out of the goodness of his heart. And perhaps Lando didn’t understand it too well because he wasn’t the one with superpowers, he wasn’t the one who felt obligated to save everyone because he knew he could 

But his boyfriend was, and he had his own thoughts about it. 

“I think you should pull away from being Spiderman.” Lando began, breaking the silence between them after a few minutes of pondering. “I’m sorry- that sounds really selfish and just bad. What I mean is, I think you should only be Spiderman when the time calls for it. There are so many crimes that this city handled before it ever had superheroes there to save the day. Things like Kraven and Doctor Octopus, of course I want you to help out. But those people contacting you for a mugging or a car chase? The police can handle that, you don’t need to be there always.

I guess what I’m trying to say is, as long as you’re doing this every day, you will never live a normal life. You’ll always be so consumed and overwhelmed with all this crime that you’ll never get to just be you. I know you love doing this at times, you love helping, but I wish you’d help yourself first. 

Sometimes I’m so terrified you won’t come home to me, that I’ll turn on the news one time and see something terrible has happened to you. I mean, look at you now! I’ll always be here to take care of you when you come home, but I couldn’t handle this everyday. I want you to be able to have a normal day, have a normal job, spend time with your friends… I love you, and I just want what’s best for you.”

He waited a few seconds for Oscar to process what he said, it was a lot, he knew. But after a minute or so, he furrowed his brows in confusion. “Oscar?” Lando gently called out, reaching and turning Oscar’s head to face him. His eyes were closed and a small snoring noise escaped the younger, exhaustion had clearly won this round. 

Lando fell back against the mattress, a loud sigh emitting from him. He could always bring it up another time. “Happy birthday, Oscar.”

*

The sunset cast its orange glowing rays over the city as Lando took off sprinting through the streets and alleyways of London, his footsteps echoing off the concrete as he pushed himself further and further away from the enemy hideout. 

The night when Oscar returned home injured, they discussed having a chat about what Lando could do to help him with his worries of not being good enough to defeat Kraven the Hunter. After a lengthy conversation, it was agreed that Lando would assist Oscar by investigating some of the hideouts and research facilities Kraven had. This was all under the condition that he’d call Oscar should he feel unsafe at any moment. 

Over the past two months, Kraven’s reign of terror over the city continued with no signs of letting up anytime soon. After extensive research and data they collected, Lando and Oscar- along with Logan- were able to figure out that he was after London’s most powerful residents, including supervillains and even Spiderman himself. 

Lando, after successfully infiltrating the last hideout and using the hardware that Logan crafted for him, was able to download the data held on the next few targets, to which Oscar ensured all targets were safe and secure. Safe places were found for them, using the support of the chief of police and Mark Webber himself. 

But this time, Lando had uncovered something further. The data he’d found this time were recorded conversations and phone calls between Kraven and his family, revealing the true purpose behind his targeted kills, why he really came to London seeking death and destruction. 

However, after downloading the necessary data, one of Kraven’s henchmen entered the room, catching him red-handed. Lando didn’t have the time to stop and contact Oscar for help, not when back-up was called instantly and he had a gun pointed at him shooting frantically. 

He barely managed to escape, hyper aware of the fact that a few of the goons were still chasing him from the moment he left the hideout, if the sound of gunshots were anything to go from. Lando was sure that they were gone by now, the goons not willing to risk being caught in the streets without Kraven to protect them and leave the hideout without any security. But he still ran, they had mechanical dogs and birds that could easily search for him, he wasn’t risking anything. 

After a few more minutes of running, he finally halted to a stop in a random alleyway, throwing his body against one of the brick walls as he allowed himself to catch his breaths. The sounds of his inhales and exhales filled the silent air. The sunset was fading away but the heat of June was still evident, his body struggling to cool down. 

Before Lando could do anything else, he grabbed his phone, sharing his location with Oscar as he sent a simple ‘here’ text to him. He scanned his surroundings, watching for any signs of potential threats. Once he was satisfied that no one had followed him and he successfully escaped, he let out a sigh of relief and his body relaxed. 

After a few minutes, he could hear a noise coming from above him, smiling brightly once he looked up and saw Spiderman suspended above him, lowering down on a web upside down. He lowered himself until their heads were level, the white eyes of the suit staring at him intently.

“Hey, Spider-boy.” Lando got out between breaths. 

“Spiderman.” Oscar was quick to correct him. “Why are you so far away from the base? And why are you out of breath?” Oscar asked suspiciously, the white eyes of the mask squinting at him.

“May have gotten caught and chased away with guns.” Lando huffed, wiping away a bed of sweat that had fallen from his forehead. “And before you yell at me for not contacting you, I couldn’t exactly say ‘hey evil henchmen, can you just wait a minute so I can tell Spiderman to come help me?’ I don’t think they’d take too well to that.” Lando quipped, well aware of what the hero was already going to say. 

He didn’t even need to see his face to know Oscar was frowning beneath the mask. “We’ll have to find a way around that, otherwise I can’t keep letting you go to these hideouts. What if something happened to you? I can’t always be there to watch… we could ask Logan to look at making something, maybe like a button or something to alert me.”

“It’s fine, we’ll look at it another time.” Lando said. “For now, I got something big.”

“Did you find the next list of targets?”

“No.” Lando shook his head. “I found out why Kraven came here, what his aim of this has been all along.”

Oscar’s white eyes of the mask widened, astonishment and pride coursing through him as Lando stood there beaming. “Well done, baby. God, I’m so proud of you right now!” 

“Kraven came to London to prove himself after he was diagnosed with cancer, he’s dying, Oscar.” Lando began to explain. “Kraven discovered he was dying and he refuses to accept a disease taking him, his whole fucked up idea is to keeping killing until he comes across something that can kill him. London is filled with so many supervillains that he decided to come here.”

“And so he’s not going to stop until he meets his match, until someone kills him…”

“Exactly! Kraven will kill until he can’t anymore.” Lando said exasperatedly. “I’ve got everything here.” He proudly displayed the hardware from Logan. “Tell me I’m amazing.”

“You are fucking incredible.” Oscar responded, disbelief coating his voice. 

“I said tell me I’m amazing!” Lando insisted, seeking out more appraisal from his boyfriend. 

“Come here.” Oscar laughed, his hand emerging to pull Lando in by his shirt. Lando really wished he could see the smile and usual heart-eyes reserved for him. “You’re amazing.” He reiterated. “I think I may actually have to ask Mark for help on this one.”

“Yeah, if his goal is just to keep killing then I think you’re going to need all the help you can get.” Lando agreed, offering him a proud smile. Oscar had trouble admitting when he needed help, and so for him to say out loud that he was going to turn to others for support unprompted was a huge step for him. “I love you, Oscar. Thank you for letting me help you.”

“If I didn’t approve of you helping, you’d have found a way to do it anyway.” Oscar retorted knowingly. 

“Now… Do I get a thank you kiss before you disappear for the evening again?” Lando asked suggestively, a playful glint in his eyes as he further closed the distance between him and Oscar’s suspended body. 

“I suppose you can have one kiss, although I’ll do my best to be home early tonight.” Oscar responded, unmoving from his position. When Lando asked for a kiss, he did expect the hero to lower himself to the ground at least, but he supposed he could make it work. 

Lando’s hands reached up, protruding his fingertips underneath the hem of the mask. He pulled it down, revealing Oscar’s plump lips and end of his nose. He watched in delight as the hero’s lips parted slightly, anticipating his next move. 

The Brit tilted his head to the side, closing the gap between their lips as they met in an awkward fashion, the angle wasn’t perfect but they made it work. Lando fully immersed himself in the moment, his fingertips delicately tracing along Oscar’s exposed skin, as if savouring the touch.

Their lips were in a dance of affection, capturing all the love that they were pouring into the kiss. The angle was special, something they’d remember as the time they kissed in a back alleyway while the sunset’s rays illuminated the red suit and tanned skin.  

A soft involuntary sound escaped Lando’s lips as Oscar’s hand found its way to the back of his neck. He gently played with the small curls, Oscar smirking into the kiss as Lando melted from the small touch. 

Lando’s tongue darted teasingly across Oscar’s lips, a playful invite to deepen the kiss. Oscar’s lips parted in response, and suddenly, their kiss evolved as their tongues explored each other desperately. The Brit could feel desire bubbling within him, the moment becoming too heated for a small alleyway kiss. 

Eventually, they broke apart, panting breathlessly, the lingering heat and want of their kiss lingering in the air. A thick wave of tension enveloped them, the Brit feeling his heartbeat in his throat. His face transformed into a confident smirk.

“Come home soon, yeah?” Lando’s voice held a seductive tone, his suggestive words hanging between them, only enticing the atmosphere further. “We can finish what we started. I’ll let you test those webs out again.” He bit his lip playfully and he smoothly rolled the hem of the Spiderman mask down, concealing the identity of the hero once more.

Oscar groaned loudly to himself, still catching his breath. “You better be ready for it.” Oscar teased, anticipation vibrating from him. “I’ll see you at home, love.” 

“See you later, Spidey-boy.” 

He quickly shot a web towards the sky, releasing the one dangling him in the air, swinging away and leaving the Brit there to his own fantasies. Lando watched him leave, and once again, admired the back end of his boyfriend through the tight suit, grinning. 

*

“Oh my god, how do I look?” Lando asked nervously, peering into his phone’s camera. His hand anxiously ran through his hair for the fifth time since their arrival, messing with the curls frantically. 

“The same answer as when you asked five minutes ago, you look great, Lando.” Oscar deadpanned, checking the time on his own phone as he waited for Lando to be satisfied with himself. 

“Handsome won’t do it. I’m meeting Mark Webber, your goddamn mentor. He’s like a dad to you! I need to look like ‘I’ll have your son home by 10pm’ and not ‘your son calls me daddy too.’” Lando whispered in a voice tinged with panic. 

After enduring a  gruelling few months of narrowly saving people from Kraven the Hunter’s ruthless terror on the city, with some people unfortunately falling victim to his crusade, the villain had finally come to an end.  With the help of Iron Man, Mark Webber, Oscar successfully defeated Kraven without it ending in death, denying him of the only thing the villain wanted. 

Kraven was arrested and transported to the maximum-security prison, the Raft, where he would spend the remainder of his days. The cancer in his body would eventually take him, which had been his biggest fear, appearing weak for succumbing to a disease. It would lead him to an early grave and the victims’ souls could rest easy.

Since then, Mark had stayed close to home, temporarily residing in London until further notice, offering assistance to Oscar should he need it. A week after the events, Mark invited Oscar over for dinner, specifically requesting to bring Lando with him. Of course, at the time the Brit jumped with joy at the opportunity, and now standing out his door, the nerves finally started to kick in as the moment drew closer. 

“First off, I do not call you daddy.” Oscar flicked Lando’s ear scoldingly, the older boy letting out a squeal of pain and shooting him an evil glare. “Secondly, Mark isn’t my dad, you don’t have to impress him. Thirdly, you look very sexy and when we’re done here, I can’t wait to tear these clothes off you and see what’s underneath.” Oscar shot him an innocent smile. 

Lando blinked in disbelief. “Mate, you can’t just say stuff like that right before we’re sitting down for a meal with Mark Webber!” Lando whined. “Let’s ditch the dinner and head back to our place.” Lando suggested hungrily as he threw his arms around Oscar’s shoulders, pulling them together. “I’m all worked up now and it’s your fault.”

Oscar shuddered, feeling the subtle arousal growing in Lando’s trousers, causing him to throw his head back with a groan. “You’re not getting out of meeting him that easy. Besides, you were the one who wanted to meet him in the first place!” He teased, a playful reminder of why they were there in the first place. 

“He’s right, Lando, Oscar’s told me how much of a big fan you are of mine. I gotta say though, he probably didn’t tell you just how excited I was to meet you too.”

The two young boys froze in place, looking at each other in fright. They slowly turned their heads towards the sound, where Mark Webber stood there in the doorway, leaning against it with his arms crossed over his chest and an amused smile. 

“Hey, Mark.” Oscar nervously chuckled to himself. 

“Hello to you too, kid.” Mark greeted, reaching out to ruffle the young Aussie’s hair despite his protests. “Lando, it’s great to meet you.” He added with a kind smile. 

“H-hello, sir.” Lando stuttered out, his mouth suddenly dry and his jaw slackened. He felt a wave of sickness wash over him as he stood in the presence of his childhood hero. “I just wanna say that I’m a huge fan, I had every Iron Man collectible growing up and I used to come to every event of yours to try and meet you! And-” Before he could continue rambling, Oscar nudged him with his elbow in the ribs, emitting a cry of pain from the Brit. 

“Damn, Lando, play it cool.” Oscar whispered to him. “You weren’t even this excited over me.” 

Mark let out a deep chuckle, clearly amused. “No, no, leave him be. Always a pleasure to meet a fan of mine. Come on in, boys. Tell me, Lando, do you have a favourite moment of mine?” He inquired the young boy. 

Lando lit up, beaming at his childhood hero. He pulled away from Oscar, following Mark into his home, who in return threw his arm over Lando’s shoulders, walking together through the big home. Oscar tutted to himself, trailing after them in disbelief. 

Eventually, they found themselves seated at the long dining table. They had helped set the table after seeing that Seb was there too, attempting to lay out the appetisers, plates and cutlery. Lando had been more than willing to help, introducing himself to Seb who loved him instantly. 

Lando and Oscar were situated side by side with Mark and Seb seated opposite them. To Lando’s surprise, he had learnt that the two older men were actually married, with Mark living in between London and New York while Seb remained in London, occasionally finding himself leaving his coffee shop and joining his husband in the states. 

Their food was soon brought out by their own personal chef, laying out multiple plates of perfectly cooked lasagna (and yes, Oscar pre-warned them of Lando’s awful picky-eating habits). Oscar, being considerate, took most of the garlic bread and generously poured it onto Lando’s plate, knowing the Brit loved it. 

Oscar smiled to himself, watching his boyfriend talk the two older men’s ears off as he overloaded them with questions and information throughout the meal. For the most part, the two seemed more than happy to indulge in his behaviour, likely pleased to know that Lando was excited to get to know them. 

“While I know you two have been together for some time now, I do think I need to ask the question.” Mark said abruptly, taking a big gulp of his whisky. He swirled the drink in his hand before glancing over to Lando. “What are your intentions with Oscar?”

Lando, who had been unfortunate enough to be sipping on his drink at the time, started having a coughing fit as he choked on the water. The young Aussie was quick to react, tapping the back of Lando’s back until his throat was cleared. The Brit turned bright red with embarrassment, angrily whispering to Oscar. “I told you he was like your goddamn dad.”

Oscar gave him a sheepish smile in return, having known full well that Mark would’ve questioned him sooner or later, feeling like he had a parental responsibility over Oscar. The last thing he wanted to do was make Lando feel nervous by telling him that, however. 

“I’m sorry, Lando. I didn’t mean to surprise you with the question. I just know how long you two have known each other and wanted to see if this is serious or not.” Mark said, but his eyes entertained a pleased glint. He hid his smile behind his glass of whisky. 

“Mark, I’m sure you don’t need to interrogate the poor boy. Surely you know Oscar is old enough to be making his own decisions, yes?” Seb interjected with an almost threatening tone, his thick German accent only making it seem all the more terrifying. 

Mark gulped and Oscar snickered to himself, the only person that had any sense of authority over Mark was the German man. “Yes, honey. I just wanted to check if he has Oscar’s intentions at heart.”

“Sir-” Lando began to talk. 

“Please, just call me Mark.” He interrupted, his hand gesturing for him to continue.

“Lando, don’t answer him.” Oscar shot Mark a glare, the man raising his hands in defence, pulling a face. 

“No, no. It’s okay, I can answer.” Lando gave him a warm smile before addressing the couple in front of him, Seb giving him a reassuring thumbs up out of the eyesight of Mark. “Mark, Oscar is my top priority in everything. I care for him and love him so deeply, and the only thing I want is to make him happy and support him in every way I can. I want nothing more than to make sure he is loved and happy.” 

Mark hummed, clearly satisfied with the answer. Oscar’s hand found Lando’s own, interlocking them together as he gave him three squeezes- the silent gesture more than enough to express his love. The Brit turned back to him, wanting nothing more than to kiss him with a surge of love, but now was not the time. 

“And I can assure you, Mark, he is amazing to me. He makes me very happy and I love him very much. Does that please the jury?” Oscar retorted to the older hero, speaking with confidence as he stared him down.

“That’s all good with me, I just wanted to check. You know how it is, Lando. His parents aren’t here and I’m the next best thing while he’s living here.”

Oscar nearly growled as he watched an entertained smirk growing on Lando’s face. “Oh really? Because when we were outside, Oscar said you were nothing like his dad. I beg to differ.”

And the table erupted into loud bickering back and forth between the pair, earning some laughter from Lando and Seb as they both gazed at their partners lovingly. Lando could see hints of themselves in the two older men, similarities in their personalities, the relationship and the dynamic between them. It filled him with joy and hope for the future, knowing that this is what their life could look like. 

“Lando, tell me more about yourself. I feel like I haven’t gotten the chance to know what you do.” Seb asked, leaning over the table and filling himself a glass of Prosecco. He poured one for Lando too, sliding it across the table and the Brit took it graciously. 

“Well, I’ve just graduated from university with an undergraduate degree in Psychology. I used to do some photography for the Daily Bugle too, but I left them back around Christmas time.” Lando responded, grimacing as he tasted the bitter Prosecco.

Seb nodded along. “And what do you plan to do now that you’re both done with university?”

“It might sound a little silly but since Christmas, I’ve actually been streaming online a few times a week, I’ve been building myself a large audience and I’m in the midst of creating my own company. I found some people who want to be part of it and we’re planning to get that up and running sometime soon. The company is called Quadrant.” Lando said shyly, feeling like the idea wouldn’ sound as good being reiterated to someone older. 

“Nonsense!” Seb exclaimed. “It does not sound silly! It is great you are so ambitious and looking to create your own business. I was the same, I first started as Mark’s personal assistant and for a while even took up wearing the Iron Man suit too. I’m not sure if you ever saw the suit, it was the blue and silver one?” Lando nodded eagerly. Of course he’d seen it, no one had ever known who the mystery second Iron Man was when seen fighting together, remaining a mystery to that very day. He felt honoured to be told such a secret. “But I gave that all up, it wasn’t for me anymore. I decided to open my own cafe, a simple business but I love it, I couldn’t ask for anything better. I still help Mark now and again with some tasks, but the coffee shop is my priority now.”

“Well, I’m really glad it worked out for you.” Lando gave him a genuine smile. 

“A little toast to your business prospering well.” Seb raised his Prosecco glass, awaiting Lando to do the same. He raised it quickly, clinking the glasses together as they each took a sip. The Brit had to hold back from gagging at the horrific taste, he made a mental note to never drink it again. 

Some time passed as they eased their way back into conversation with Oscar and Mark, talking for what seemed hours, long passed the desert being brought out and devoured. 

Mark cleared his throat, downed the rest of his whisky and fixed Oscar with a serious expression. The room’s atmosphere shifted, Seb placing his drink down on the table and placing his hands in his lap. “I’ll be honest with you, I had ulterior motives for inviting you to dinner tonight. There was something I wanted to discuss with you.” Mark declared, his words hanging in the air. 

“What is it, Mark? Is everything okay?” Oscar asked with a worried look on his face, learning forward instinctively to get closer to Mark. 

The older Aussie laughed, shaking his head lightly. “Nothing’s wrong, don’t worry your little head, kid. I just wanted to say, I’m retiring.”

A few beats of silence, Oscar taking his time to process the words. “You’re retiring?” He questioned, disbelief evident in his tone. 

Mark nodded, Seb reaching over to take his husband’s hand and give it a squeeze. God, Lando truly couldn’t get over how similar they were to the younger couple. He’d be paying more attention to it if he himself wasn’t frozen in shock at hearing his childhood hero, Iron Man, was giving up the mantle. 

“I’m retiring.” Mark repeated. “I’ve done my time, kid. I’ve been saving this city, other cities, this world and other worlds for years now. I’ve given my all to being Iron Man and as much as I love it, I deserve the time to myself now. I want to start a family.” He smiled at his husband . “I want to live my life without worrying what danger I’ve gotta sort out next. Webber Industries will continue and I’ll still be the CEO, but I was hoping to have a partner in the business.” His tone was suggestive. 

Oscar’s face morphed with realisation, putting together the puzzle pieces as Mark spoke. “Are you asking me to run Webber Industries with you?”

“If you’ll have me.” Mark said. “There’s nobody else I want by my side running the business with me. You’re magnificent, extraordinary past your age. You don’t have to accept it now, you can have a think about it.”

“I’ll do it.” Oscar responded, his decision already decided with a determined face. 

“That easy?”

“That easy.” He repeated.

Mark nodded thoughtfully. “Seb will draw up a contract for you soon then, it’s a pleasure doing business with you, Mr Piastri. You’re officially welcome to the Webber Industries family.” 

“Oscar.” Lando whispered, his voice filled with awe. “This is incredible for you. I mean- oh my god, you’re going to be running Webber Industries!”

“This is honestly such a dream come true.” Oscar gushed, holding Lando’s face in his hands as he planted a kiss on his lips, not a care in the world for the couple watching them. 

“Ah, ah, ah! I’ll be running it with him, and he won’t be completely running it just yet. I’ll be showing him the reins and stuff first, and I know he’ll still be working hard as Spiderman, protecting the city.” Mark voiced, interrupting their moment. “How are you feeling about my retirement then? No objections? If you ever really needed it and it was life or death, I’d always put it back on for you. Just remember that.”

“Well- it’s a bit of a shock to the system, I won’t pretend. But I understand it, you’ve been doing this for so long and it’s about time you put yourself first. Don’t worry, I’ve got the city, it’s safe in my hands.” Oscar declared with pride.

And with that, all the excitement Lando felt for his boyfriend disappeared, replaced with an unsettling feeling growing in the pit of his stomach. His face fell, unable to feign happiness at the news any longer. For so long now, Lando had been hoping and praying that he’d have the opportunity to bring up the conversation with Oscar- to suggest that he pull away from being Spiderman so often and finally prioritise himself. He had tried it all those months ago on Oscar’s birthday, but the Aussie had collapsed with exhaustion after suffering a horrible injury, further proving Lando’s point.

He couldn’t shake the bitterness, especially when all he wanted was for Oscar to put himself first, finally start taking care of his well-being. Lando had sworn he’d always be there for him, always there to pick up the pieces and help him with whatever he needed and he still planned to, but he wanted his boyfriend to finally give himself a well deserved break. 

Mark, ever the observant, saw Lando’s face crumble at Oscar’s words. He recognised the feeling, he saw it in Seb’s eyes years ago when he threw himself into his work. He remembered how Seb would look at him after nearly losing his life to a purple intergalactic titan, he still fought and put his life on the line. Maybe that’s why he felt the urge to say something, he saw too much of himself and Seb in the couple, and he wanted them to live as they did. 

“As much as I- and everyone in London- appreciate all the hard work you’ve done for the city over the last few years, I believe you need to take a step back yourself.” Mark spoke up, watching as the smile slid off of Oscar’s face. 

“What do you mean? This city needs a hero, now more than ever if you’re going to be retiring.” Oscar retorted, his shoulders tensing as he dropped Lando’s hand. The Brit tried to hide the hurt look on his face, looking at his now empty hand. 

“And I’m not saying you have to stop.” Mark reassured him. “But- I don’t think you need to be there for everything anymore. You’ve given them your whole life for the past few years, I’m surprised you actually managed to graduate! There’s obviously times you need to be there, like with Kraven. But you and I both know that there’s things that the police could handle, you don’t need to be there for every crime.”

“But what if they do need me? What if things turn south and they need me there?”

“Oscar, before us, how do you think they managed before?”

“I-” He was dumbstruck, Mark had a valid point. Lando watched Oscar’s face, conflict evident as he processed Mark’s words thoughtfully. “If anything were to happen that I knew I could’ve prevented, I’d never forgive myself.”

“Oscar.” Seb spoke softly, reaching across the table and taking Oscar’s hand in his own. “Not every life needs to rest upon your shoulders, you are not responsible for every soul in this city.” 

Those words seemed to particularly strike Oscar, the young Aussie taking a deep breath as he thought about how his life could be if he finally took a step back from the hero lifestyle. For years, it was all he knew, he lived and breathed as Spiderman. 

“Oscar, you’ve done so well for the last few years, but you deserve to live your life now the way you want. You have so much going for you, so much you can do if you just put yourself first.” Lando added on, his voice almost pleading him to listen. 

His eyes met Lando’s own, searching for an answer to it all in his eyes. Oscar needn’t ask what his thoughts were, he could feel it in the beats of the Brit’s heart and the look on his face, he wanted Oscar to care for himself. He envisioned a life with Lando, free from having to run away multiple times a day and free to love his boyfriend how he wishes, to take him on dates, to spend so much more time with him. He could spend more time with his friends, heck, he could even travel back to Australia to see his family. 

The answer seemed so obvious, but to allow himself to accept it seemed so much harder than anything he’d ever faced. 

Oscar’s hand sought Lando’s, connecting them once more and giving him three squeezes, to which they were eagerly returned. He knew what he needed to do, but it would take some time. “I’ll try. I’ll give it a go for us. I can’t promise I’ll be perfect, and that I won’t be running off in the middle of the night hoping to be of some use, but I’ll do my best.” 

Lando beamed at him and Oscar knew he looked just as in love as Lando did at that moment. “That’s all I really want, I know you won't drop it all and it’ll take time, but that’s okay. We’ll figure it out together, yeah?”

“We always do.” Oscar smiled at him lovingly, nodding along to Lando’s words. 

The room felt lighter, tensions easing as they finally acknowledged the barrier between them. Mark and Seb exchanged knowing glances, reminding them of themselves more and more with each and every gesture. 

“All settled?” Oscar nodded, Mark gave him a curt nod in return. “Brilliant, now let’s get into details about Webber Industries, so-”

And the night carried on, they continued with what was left of the dinner, the conversation shifting from plans for the future to lighter topics. The sound of laughter filled the air as they all shared fond stories of one another. 

And as Lando turned to Oscar and saw his nose and eyes scrunched in laughter, his bunny teeth on perfect display, he knew there was nowhere else he’d rather be.

*

The Australian sunset painted the sky in hues of orange and red as Lando and Oscar sat on Melbourne's beach atop their orange blanket, making its way all the way from the city of London. The distant sound of seagulls sounded with the ocean releasing the melody of its waves. The beach was relatively busy, everyone there to watch the sunset with the young couple. 

Dressed in swim trunks, Lando and Oscar found peace resting at the beach after having already spent most of the day there. After a discussion with Oscar previously about stepping away from being Spiderman all the time, he’d finally heed the advice and started pulling away from it, bit by bit. It was taking some time, and it took a lot of convincing from Lando to book some time away in Australia, spending the holidays this year with the Aussie’s family. 

It was past Christmas, past their one year anniversary and the two couldn’t be happier. They had gone through so much together in the last year and their connection and love had only grown stronger. 

Lando had finally started his own business, employing the help of Seb in making sure he was doing everything correctly. He was in the midst of creating some real life content with the members of Quadrant, with some big events planned for the next year. It was all going well, with it only growing in popularity each day and he had already started to make some good money from it. He also helped out down Seb’s cafe at times when he found himself with free time, taking quite a liking to the German man. 

Oscar was officially Mark’s business partner in Webber Industries, proving to already be a valuable asset to the company. He was ambitious, career driven and always looking to create something that will help the world. Mark was still watching over him, but he was pleased with the work Oscar had done so far. And he still loved being Spiderman, but he enjoyed being Oscar a lot more now too. He’d always be there if he was needed, but he was loving his new found freedom. 

“It’s so weird to think we’ve been together over a year now.” Lando mused, his voice a gentle murmur, his gaze fixed on the horizon as the sunset slowly started to melt along the sealine. “It’s crazy to think about how it all started.”

Oscar nodded in agreement, his eyes softened as he turned his attention to Lando. “Well, time flies by when you’re living life with the person you love.” He said with a tender smile. The older boy scoffed at his sweet words, scooting closer to Oscar until they were pressed together. He leant his head on Oscar’s shoulder, shuddering when an arm wrapped around him. “I couldn't ask for a better ending for us, I’m so happy for how it all turned out.”

“Hey! This isn’t the end, Oscar!” Lando poked his tongue out at him as he protested. “This is just the beginning of our story.”

“You’re right.” He hummed. “But like I said, I’m glad to be doing this life with you.”

“And in every world, in every outcome, you’re always doing life with me.”

“God, surely I’ll get sick of you at one point?” Oscar nudged him with his shoulder, grinning at the feigned annoyed expression on his boyfriend’s face. 

“Yeah, yeah, come here and kiss me before I break up with you.” Lando retorted, gazing at his lips before Oscar wordlessly closed the gap. It was short and sweet, enough to still feel the tingles and butterflies he usually died. His hand was on Lando’s cheek, stroking at the even more tanned than usual complexion with such love. Lando let out a little giggle to himself, looking at the sun kissed cheeks and nose of Oscar. 

“I love you, Lando.” Their hands locked together seamlessly, always reaching for each other, three squeezes. 

“I love you too, Oscar.” Three squeezes back. “It’s you and me, forever and always.” 

The ground suddenly shook beneath them, causing ripples in the ocean as it moved sporadically. The sand beneath them seemed to come alive with movement, little grains bouncing in the air as the world around them started to shift. 

Green mist started to grow and shimmer in the air, offering a sinister energy, an evil cackle following closely behind it. A figure appeared within the mist, its hands pouring out the green mist relentlessly. They were wearing a fish-bowl like helmet, completely glazed over with nothing within. The people on the beach let out screams as the world moved abnormally around them, a big tidal wave from the ocean seeming to be looming over them. 

Oscar sighed, Spiderman duties called again, even in Australia. How he’d get away with Spiderman appearing in another country halfway across the world was a problem for future him. Right then, at that moment, he had a job to do. 

“I’m so sorry, Lando.” Oscar apologised, already releasing his hold on Lando as he stood up from the ground. “I need you to get to safety, okay? I love you so much.” 

Lando gave him an all knowing look, standing up and grabbing the orange blanket with him. “I love you too. Don’t apologise though, Oscar. I knew what I was signing myself up to.”

“You are truly incredible.” He planted one last kiss on Lando's lips, praying for good luck.

Lando returned the affection with a warm and loving smile. “You mean amazing. Now, go save them, Spidey.”
